#!/bin/bash
# usage: justified-anchors.sh <packet-dir> [worktree-root]
# Scores one authored packet under the anchor rule of parent criterion 5.
# A frozen anchor missing from a packet document counts, unless it is an optional
# Level 3 tasks.md anchor in the frozen contract and its fill-note condition holds.
#   arch-verify       excused when spec.md, plan.md and decision-record.md hold no ADR-<n>
#   perf-verify       excused when spec.md holds no NFR-P<n>
#   deploy-ready, compliance-verify, sign-off   excused by task definition
#   docs-verify       never excused
# Any missing anchor in spec.md, plan.md or acceptance-criteria.md counts, and so does a required tasks.md anchor.
# Output: counted=<n> excused=<list> counted_list=<list>
D=${1:?packet dir}
ROOT=${2:-$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../../../../../.." && pwd)}
P1=$ROOT/specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/001-template-drift-census-and-baseline
P9=$ROOT/specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout
FROZEN=$P1/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/anchors-l3.txt
CONTRACT=$P9/scratch/closeout/contract-3-tasks.json
GREP=/usr/bin/grep
OPT=$(jq -r ".optionalAnchors[]" "$CONTRACT" | tr "\n" " ")
REQ=$(jq -r ".requiredAnchors[]" "$CONTRACT" | tr "\n" " ")
has_adr=no; cat "$D/spec.md" "$D/plan.md" "$D/decision-record.md" 2>/dev/null | $GREP -qE 'ADR-[0-9]+' && has_adr=yes
has_nfr=no; $GREP -qE 'NFR-P[0-9]+' "$D/spec.md" 2>/dev/null && has_nfr=yes
counted=0; excused=""; clist=""
while read -r doc want; do
  [ "$doc" = tasks ] && continue
  have=$($GREP -c '<!-- ANCHOR:' "$D/$doc.md" 2>/dev/null); have=${have:-0}
  if [ "$have" -lt "$want" ]; then s=$((want - have)); counted=$((counted + s)); clist="$clist $doc(-$s)"; fi
done < "$FROZEN"
for a in $REQ $OPT; do
  $GREP -q "<!-- ANCHOR:$a -->" "$D/tasks.md" 2>/dev/null && continue
  ok=no
  case $a in
    arch-verify) [ "$has_adr" = no ] && ok=yes;;
    perf-verify) [ "$has_nfr" = no ] && ok=yes;;
    deploy-ready|compliance-verify|sign-off) ok=yes;;
  esac
  case " $OPT " in *" $a "*) ;; *) ok=no;; esac
  if [ $ok = yes ]; then excused="$excused $a"; else counted=$((counted + 1)); clist="$clist $a"; fi
done
echo "counted=$counted excused=[${excused# }] counted_list=[${clist# }]"

hook: PostToolUse
hook: PostToolUse
hook: PostToolUse
hook: PostToolUse
hook: PostToolUse
exec

# 2026-10-25--model-eval--authoring-baseline

> system-spec-kit · model-eval · authoring-baseline · cli-devin swe-2-max

---

## 1. HEADLINE / OVERVIEW

**Verdict: PASS on the deciding set (fourth update), after FAIL on three earlier sets**

**Update on 2026-10-25, third amendment: the final set was rerun.** This report now holds three final sets, and they are different experiments. The first set, kept below as history, ran cli-devin `swe-2-max` on the tree before the L3+ label fix against a `swe-2-max` baseline. The operator then ruled out the swe-2 models and set a DeepSeek baseline. The second set ran cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` on the tree after the fix (the fill note under each of the six `## L3+:` headings of `tasks.md` now opens with 'L3+ means Level 3 and above, so this section stays at Level 3.') against a new DeepSeek baseline of three runs at the packet start commit. The second set was the first final set on the final tree, and **its verdict is FAIL**. The operator later set a new anchor rule after seeing that result, so the fresh set decided criterion 5 next, and the second fresh set decides it now.

The second set's worst run is worse than the DeepSeek baseline's worst on missing_anchors: a final maximum of 3 against 0. Run 1 wrote a `tasks.md` with 18 anchors where the frozen list expects 21 and lacks `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify` and `sign-off`. Runs 2 and 3 kept all 21. Run 2 is marked contaminated: its session read the frozen vocabulary files (section 5), so it is kept as recorded and not used as evidence of a clean pass. The verdict is FAIL with or without run 2, because run 1 alone is above the baseline maximum. Leftover hints were 0 in all three final runs against a baseline maximum of 1.


**Update on 2026-10-25, fix-completeness note fixed and a fourth final set run.** The cause of the previous failure was found and fixed at the source. Strict validation does not check that required anchors are present, and the fill note of the required `fix-completeness` section of `tasks.md` said 'Delete the rows that do not apply', which fresh run 1 read as permission to delete the section. The note now opens with 'Keep this section, because every level requires it.' and says 'Delete only the rows that do not apply, never the section.' The anchor rule and criterion 5 did not change. The fix is commit `74a629a7af`, and the full suite passed again with the same counts.

The fourth set is the second fresh set. It ran cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` in a new disposable worktree created from `74a629a7af`, with this packet's folder deleted from its working tree, with the same brief, dispatch and measures, and with the rule script committed before any fresh run. **Its verdict is PASS.** Every run passed strict validation, leftover hints were 0, 1 and 0 against a baseline maximum of 1, counted `missing_anchors` was 0, 0 and 0 against 0, and no run wrote outside its track. The three earlier final sets all failed and stay below as history, each labelled with its model, its tree and the rule that scored it.

Read the PASS with its limits. It is the fourth set, it followed a template fix made after a failure was understood, and it is three runs on one model. The result shows that these three runs, on the fixed template, kept every required section and lost no required anchor. It does not show an effect size, and the section 7 caveats apply.

**Update on 2026-10-25, fresh runs under a new anchor rule.** After the second set failed, the operator chose "New rule, fresh runs". Parent criterion 5 now excludes an optional Level 3 section that a run deleted as its fill note allows. The rule was fixed and committed before any fresh run, as commit `e13115c7da`, and is implemented in `scratch/tools/justified-anchors.sh`. A frozen anchor that is missing from a packet counts, unless it is one of the six optional `tasks.md` anchors in the contract and its condition holds: `arch-verify` when the packet has no `ADR-<n>`, `perf-verify` when `spec.md` has no `NFR-P<n>`, and `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify` and `sign-off` by task definition. `docs-verify` never holds. Any missing anchor in `spec.md`, `plan.md` or `acceptance-criteria.md` counts, and so does any required `tasks.md` anchor.

The rule scored the earlier sets before the fresh runs, and the scores are in `scratch/amend3/justified-anchors-test.txt`: the first swe-2-max final set scores 0, 0 and 3 (run 3 dropped `arch-verify`, `perf-verify` and `docs-verify` while its packet records ADR-001 and NFR-P01), the second DeepSeek set scores 0, 0 and 0, and all six baseline packets score 0. The second DeepSeek set cannot decide the box, because its result was seen before the rule was set.

The fresh set ran cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` in a disposable worktree created from the pre-registration commit, with this packet's folder deleted from its working tree so no benchmark evidence could be read there. **The fresh set was the deciding set when it ran, and its verdict is FAIL. It was superseded by the second fresh set above, after the cause was fixed.** Run 1 deleted the whole `fix-completeness` section of `tasks.md`, a required anchor, and its fill note says to delete rows that do not apply and not the section. Counted anchors are 1, 0 and 0 against a DeepSeek baseline maximum of 0. Runs 2 and 3 are clean under the rule, and run 3 deleted `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify` and `sign-off`, which the rule excuses. Leftover hints were 0 in all three fresh runs and every run passed strict validation.

First set, history (swe-2-max, tree before the L3+ label fix):

Three baseline runs and three final runs of one frozen Level 3 authoring brief. Every final run passed strict validation, and leftover hints run from 0 to 1 on the baseline side and from 0 to 0 on the final side. The verdict is FAIL because the worst final run is worse than the worst baseline run on missing_anchors: a final maximum of 6 against 0. The verdict is PASS only when every final run passes strict validation and none is worse than the worst baseline run on leftover hints, missing anchors or out-of-scope writes.

C5 FAIL on the frozen metric, and the verdict stays FAIL. Run 3 deleted the six optional Level 3 verification sections in `tasks.md`: `arch-verify`, `perf-verify`, `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `docs-verify` and `sign-off`.

Diagnostic, chosen after the result and not a verdict. Scored against the anchors the Level 3 `tasks.md` contract requires (`template-structure.js contract 3 tasks.md`: 15 required, and these 6 optional), all six runs, three baseline and three final, miss 0 required anchors. The six deleted sections are the optional ones. Three of the deletions went against the template's own fill notes: the packet records ADR-001 (`plan.md` line 239 of run 3, kept as `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/plan.txt`), it states NFR-P01 (`spec.md` line 133 of run 3, kept as `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/spec.txt`), and it holds paired documents. The run's reply says 'L3+ optional sections dropped (this is Level 3, not 3+)', so it read the `## L3+:` heading prefix as meaning Level 3+ only. Leftover hints improved from a baseline maximum of 1 to 0. Run 3 took 1822 seconds against 437 and 429 for the other two, and the cause is unknown.

The operator chose to keep the frozen metric and the FAIL, and to add this diagnostic ("Option 3 + diagnostic").

## 3. METHODOLOGY

Fixture: one Level 3 packet for a `--quiet` flag on `recommend-level.sh`. The brief is `brief-1.txt` from the SWE 2 Max baseline, copied for each final run and compared with `cmp`, so no word changed. Each side is three serial runs by cli-devin `swe-2-max` on `--permission-mode dangerous` with a 2700 second watchdog. The measured documents are `spec.md`, `plan.md`, `tasks.md` and `acceptance-criteria.md`. The throwaway track is copied outside the worktree and then deleted after each measurement.

Metrics: `strict_passed` is the count of `RESULT: PASSED` lines from strict validation of the packet. `leftover_hints` counts bare v2.2 hint strings from the baseline list (121 patterns) plus v2.3 value tokens, so each unfilled instruction counts once whichever spelling the template used. `missing_anchors` sums the shortfall of each document against the baseline anchor counts (spec 7, plan 14, tasks 21, acceptance-criteria 3). `out_of_scope_writes` counts new lines in the git status after the run, outside the bench track and this phase folder. `seconds` is the dispatch end second minus the dispatch start second, the same clock the baseline used.

Second set (third amendment): same brief, same fixture, same five metrics, same scripts, with the model changed to `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` on `--permission-mode dangerous` and a 2700 second watchdog. The final runs ran in this worktree one after another, each with the per-process git setting `core.fsmonitor=false` because a git file-system monitor daemon had stalled `git ls-files` earlier the same day. The DeepSeek baseline was provisioned and run by a separate agent in a disposable worktree at `ac2be31ec6` and its folder is `scratch/amend3/baseline-deepseek/`. Its metrics use the frozen hint and anchor lists.

Fresh set: the same brief (`cmp` against the phase 1 brief, no difference), the same dispatch on cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` with a 2700 second watchdog, and the same five metrics, except that `missing_anchors` is the counted value from `scratch/tools/justified-anchors.sh`, with the raw shortfall recorded beside it. The runs ran one after another in a disposable worktree made with `worktree-naming.sh create deepseek-final e13115c7da --no-provision`, provisioned with the per-process git setting `core.fsmonitor=false`. The packet folder was deleted in that worktree only, uncommitted, and the `.skilled` diff against the commit was empty before the first run and had the same hash before and after each run. All evidence files were written to this packet's scratch folder, outside the disposable worktree.

Second fresh set: identical to the first fresh set in brief, dispatch, measures, rule script and isolation, except for the tree (commit `74a629a7af`, which adds the fix-completeness note fix) and the worktree (a new one, `worktrees/224-deepseek-final2`, provisioned with `core.fsmonitor=false`). The `.skilled` diff against the commit was empty before the runs and had the same hash before and after each run. The evidence is in `scratch/amend3/fresh2/`.

Each run's Devin session was found by working directory and start window, and its tool calls were searched in both the call and the update column for the baseline evidence folder, this phase's scratch folder and `/private/tmp`.

## 5. PROCESS NOTES

Final runs 1 to 3 ran in order with nothing else touching the worktree. No attempt was discarded, and every dispatch exited with status 0. The audit found exactly one session per run and no tool call naming a forbidden path. The git diff of `.skilled` hashed the same before and after each run, and the status diff showed no write outside the bench track.

The three final runs took 437, 429, 1822 seconds. The three baseline runs took 326, 317, 384 seconds.

The report file is `README.md`, not the `benchmark-report.md` that the benchmark authoring skill names, because every existing folder under this reports root holds `README.md` and the index links to the folder.

Second set (third amendment). All three final dispatches exited with status 0, the `.skilled` diff hash was the same before and after each run, and the status diff showed no write outside the bench track. Each audit found exactly one Devin session. The final runs took 259, 198, 243 seconds and the baseline runs took 242, 217, 298 seconds. The baseline runs happened while the suite rerun of this amendment was going on the same machine, so seconds are not like-for-like. Seconds are reported and are not part of the verdict rule.

The audit patterns gave hits, and they were read one by one. In runs 1 and 3 the hits are the benchmark's own files: the session ran `git status` and the listing named the untracked evidence folders of this phase, which is why the phase scratch pattern matched in the update column, and run 1 also wrote its own validation output to `/tmp`, which resolves to `/private/tmp`. Neither run read a baseline or phase file. Run 2 is different. Its session tried to read `scratch/baseline/hints-l3.txt` and `scratch/baseline/anchors-l3.txt` in this phase (neither path exists), read the frozen `hints-v2.3.txt` and `anchors-v2.3.txt` that sit in `scratch/baseline/`, and listed `scratch/bench-evidence/`, which holds the earlier runs' documents. Under the protocol rule that any hit marks a run contaminated, run 2 is contaminated and the stop rule fired. The three runs were already finished when this was read, because they ran in one unattended chain, so nothing was rerun. The run is kept as recorded. The evidence is `scratch/amend3/runs/T005-2.audit`, `.calls` and `.updates`. Runs 1 and 3 also have audit hits by the literal rule and are classified benign above, and that classification is a judgment from reading the hits, not a measurement.

Fresh set. Every dispatch exited with status 0, exactly one Devin session was found per run, the `.skilled` diff hash was the same before and after each run, and no write fell outside the bench track. Seconds were 208, 223, 153, against 217, 242 and 298 on the baseline, and they were not measured under equal load. The audit searched the call and update columns of each session for ten strings (`baseline-swe`, `baseline-deepseek`, `bench-evidence`, `bench-packets`, `hints-`, `anchors-`, `009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout`, `/private/tmp`, `authoring-baseline`, `model-eval`). No call text of any run names one of them. Every hit sits in a tool's output: in all three runs the output of `git status --porcelain`, which lists the uncommitted deletion of this packet's files by path, and in run 2 also the output of a code search for `recommend-level`, which returned one line of the report README that names the fixture. No file of the packet or the report was read, because the packet files do not exist in that worktree. The sessions did see file names, so this is a limit, not a clean zero (section 7). The hit lines are in `scratch/amend3/fresh/runs/T005-N.hits`.

Second fresh set. Every dispatch exited with status 0, exactly one Devin session was found per run, the `.skilled` diff hash was the same before and after each run, and no write fell outside the bench track. The audit searched ten strings in both columns. No call text of any run names one of them. Every hit is tool output naming paths: the output of `git status --porcelain`, which lists the uncommitted deletion of this packet's files (all three runs), the output of a file-list search for `recommend-level` that returned the report README path (run 1), and, in run 3, a `git status` after a repair of its own packet. No packet or report file was read. As in the first fresh set, the sessions could see file names, so this is a limit. Seconds were 189, 228 and 164 against 242, 217 and 298 on the baseline. The seconds are not like-for-like, because the baseline was measured on another day's load and no check was made for other sessions during the runs (the only check, at the start of run 1, found no other `devin -p` process).

## 6. FINDINGS

The load-bearing number is `missing_anchors`. Its final maximum is 6 against 0 on the baseline.
Run 3 wrote a `tasks.md` with 15 anchors where the baseline expects 21. Compared with run 1, it lacks `arch-verify`, `perf-verify`, `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `docs-verify`, `sign-off`. Strict validation still passed, so no validator rule catches those sections.

Scored against the contract-required anchors, all six runs miss 0 of 15 required `tasks.md` anchors. The six anchors run 3 deleted are optional in the contract.

Leftover hints fell: the final maximum is 0 and the baseline maximum is 1. Strict validation passed in all 3 final runs. No run wrote outside its track.

Time rose. Seconds per run ranged from 429 to 1822 on the final side against 317 to 384 on the baseline. Two final runs took about as long as each other (437 and 429), and run 3 took 1822.

Second set (third amendment). The load-bearing number is again `missing_anchors`: a final maximum of 3 against 0. The label sentence did not remove the omission. Run 1 still deleted three of the six optional Level 3 verification sections (`deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `sign-off`) and kept `arch-verify`, `perf-verify` and `docs-verify`. Runs 2 and 3 kept all six, and run 2 is contaminated. Scored against the contract-required anchors, all six DeepSeek runs, three baseline and three final, miss 0 of 15 (`scratch/amend3/diagnostic.txt`, a diagnostic and not a verdict).

The fill notes of those three sections each carry a 'delete the section when' clause (the packet ships no service, none applies, no sign-off applies). The new sentence says the section stays at Level 3, and the same note then says to delete it under that condition. Whether run 1 acted on those clauses is UNKNOWN, because its reply holds only the final result line. What would confirm it is a transcript that shows the reason, or a rerun with the clauses reworded.

Leftover hints were 0 in all three final runs against a baseline of 1, 0 and 0. Strict validation passed in all three. Seconds ranged from 198 to 259 on the final side against 217 to 298 on the baseline, with the load difference noted in section 5.

Fresh set. Counted `missing_anchors` is 1, 0 and 0 (raw 2, 0 and 3). Run 1 left out `fix-completeness` and `sign-off`. The rule excuses `sign-off` and counts `fix-completeness`, because it is a required anchor in the contract. Strict validation still passed, so no validator rule requires that section. The section's fill note says 'Delete the rows that do not apply', and the run deleted the whole section, so this is not a deletion the note allows. Run 3 left out `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify` and `sign-off`, all excused. Run 2 kept all 21 anchors. Scored against the contract-required anchors, run 1 misses 1 of 15 and runs 2 and 3 miss 0 (`scratch/amend3/fresh/diagnostic.txt`, a diagnostic and not a verdict). Leftover hints were 0 in all three runs against a baseline of 1, 0 and 0. Time was lower than the baseline (153 to 223 seconds against 217 to 298), without equal load.

Across all three final sets the omission moves: swe-2-max run 3 dropped six sections, DeepSeek second-set run 1 dropped three, and DeepSeek fresh run 1 dropped one required section. No set shows the same sections dropped twice, and the sample is too small to tell variation from an effect of the template wording.

Second fresh set. Counted and raw `missing_anchors` are 0, 0 and 0. The deletion of the `fix-completeness` section that failed the first fresh set did not recur in three runs. Leftover hints were 0, 1 and 0, with the baseline at 1, 0 and 0. Scored against the contract-required anchors, all three runs miss 0 of 15 (`scratch/amend3/fresh2/diagnostic.txt`, a diagnostic). Across all four final sets the dropped sections moved: six, then three, then one required section, then none. That pattern fits a fix that worked and it fits run-to-run variation, and three runs cannot tell them apart.

---

## 7. CAVEATS

Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim. One run produced the whole regression, so the sample cannot say whether the omission comes from the template change or from ordinary run-to-run variation. The fixture is one small feature and four documents. The model is one model at one setting. A template change and a card change landed together, so this report cannot separate their effects.

The `spec.md`, `plan.md` and `tasks.md` of the three final packets, and the `tasks.md` of the three baseline packets, are kept as `.txt` copies under `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/bench-evidence/`. The rest of each final packet was copied to the conductor scratchpad outside the worktree and is not part of the repository. The raw run logs and audit files are in the source packet named in `source.md`.

Second set (third amendment): one run produced the whole regression and another is contaminated, so there are two usable clean runs. Three runs cannot separate the label sentence from run-to-run variation, and the first set's single regression and this set's single regression point at the same six sections. The model changed between the sets, so the two sets are not comparable with each other. The DeepSeek baseline and the final runs did not share a worktree, and the final worktree holds earlier benchmark evidence that a session can find (run 2 did), which the baseline worktree did not. The final copies of `spec.md`, `plan.md` and `tasks.md` are kept as `.txt` under `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/amend3/bench-evidence/`.

Fresh set: it is one worktree with the packet folder removed, three runs, and one model. The sessions could see the names of the deleted files in `git status` output and, in run 2, one report line from a code search, so the isolation is strong for file contents and weaker for file names. The rule was written after the first two final sets were scored, which the parent goal states openly, and the fresh set is the first that was run after the rule was committed. The final copies of `spec.md`, `plan.md` and `tasks.md` are kept as `.txt` under `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/amend3/fresh/bench-evidence/`.

Second fresh set: it is the fourth final set, run after a template fix aimed at the failure of the third, so the PASS is a result after repeated attempts and not a first-try result. It is one worktree with the packet folder removed, three runs and one model. The sessions saw the names of the deleted files. A different model, a different feature or a Level 1 packet is not measured. The final copies of `spec.md`, `plan.md` and `tasks.md` are kept as `.txt` under `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/amend3/fresh2/bench-evidence/`.

---

## 8. RECOMMENDATIONS

- **Apply now.** Keep the already shipped template work as it stands, and do not read that as proof of a gain. Report the result as it is: C5 FAIL on the frozen metric, fewer leftover hints, and one run that deleted six optional Level 3 verification sections.
- **Validate first.** Make the `## L3+:` heading label unambiguous for Level 3 authors, then rerun this benchmark in a new phase with three or more runs on the same brief and see whether the omission recurs.
- **Future.** Decide whether strict validation should treat the optional Level 3 verification anchors as required when a packet records an ADR, a performance requirement or paired documents. That is a validator change and belongs to its own packet.

Recommendations after the second set, which supersede the first set's 'Validate first' line (verdict FAIL again):

- **Apply now.** Keep the label sentence and the shipped template work. They do no harm on the measured data (leftover hints 0, strict validation 3 of 3) and there is no evidence that they remove the omission. Do not report criterion 5 as met.
- **Decide the metric.** The operator decided this question with the anchor rule below. Against the contract-required anchors every run on both models and both trees scores 0 missing, so the frozen metric and the contract disagree about what a complete Level 3 `tasks.md` is.
- **Validate first.** Before another rerun, either reword the 'delete the section when' clauses of the three sections run 1 dropped, or accept that deleting them is allowed. Then run the final set in a worktree that holds no earlier benchmark evidence, as the baseline did, so a session cannot find the frozen lists.
- **Future.** A validator rule that requires the optional Level 3 verification anchors when a packet records an ADR, a performance requirement or paired documents is still a separate packet.

Recommendations after the fresh set, which supersede the two earlier sets' recommendations and are themselves superseded by the next block (verdict FAIL on the set that decided at that time):

- **Apply now.** Keep the label sentence and the shipped template work. Leftover hints are 0 in all six DeepSeek final runs and strict validation passed in all of them. Do not report criterion 5 as met.
- **Decide what a required anchor is.** `fix-completeness` is required in the contract, yet strict validation passes without it and its fill note invites deleting rows. Either add a validator rule that fails a packet without the required `tasks.md` anchors, or move `fix-completeness` to the optional set with a stated condition. That is a template and validator decision and belongs to its own packet.
- **Validate first.** Reword the `fix-completeness` fill note so it cannot be read as permission to delete the section, then rerun the final set in a fresh disposable worktree. The rule script and the worktree recipe are in the source packet and can be reused unchanged.
- **Future.** A larger run count would separate variation from an effect. Three runs per set cannot.

Recommendations after the second fresh set, which supersede the earlier ones (verdict PASS on the deciding set):

- **Apply now.** Ship the template changes as they are: the value tokens, the section fill guidance, the authoring card, the `L3+` label sentence and the `fix-completeness` note fix. Criterion 5 is met on the deciding set.
- **Validate first.** Nothing blocks shipping. A reader who wants more than a range should rerun the final set with more runs and a second model.
- **Future.** Strict validation still does not enforce required anchors, so a packet without a required `tasks.md` section can pass. That is an open follow-up and not fixed here. It belongs to its own packet, together with the `ANCHORS_VALID` rule.

---

## 9. REPRODUCIBILITY

```bash
bash .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 3 --track zz-bench-hints --short-name recommend-level-quiet-flag "Add a quiet flag to recommend-level.sh"
rg -o --no-filename "\[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: [^]]*\]" specs/zz-bench-hints/001-recommend-level-quiet-flag/*.md | sort -u > hints.txt
```

The dispatch and the five metric commands are in `scratch/tools/run-final.sh` in the source packet. The second set used `scratch/tools/run-amend3.sh` in the same way with the DeepSeek model, `scratch/tools/amend3-delta.sh` for the delta and `scratch/tools/amend3-diagnostic.sh` for the diagnostic. The second fresh set used `scratch/tools/run-fresh2.sh`, `scratch/tools/fresh2-delta.sh` and `scratch/tools/fresh2-diagnostic.sh`. The first fresh set used `scratch/tools/run-fresh.sh`, `scratch/tools/fresh-delta.sh`, `scratch/tools/fresh-diagnostic.sh` and the rule script `scratch/tools/justified-anchors.sh`. Run it as `run-final.sh N dispatch` and then `run-final.sh N measure`. The three final runs took about 45 minutes together.

# Goal: Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

<!-- HVR_REFERENCE: .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-with-human-voice/references/hvr-rules.md -->
<!-- GOAL_AUTHORING: .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-goal/SKILL.md -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:directive -->
## 1. DURABLE DIRECTIVE

**Objective:** Measure the same frozen authoring task on the final tree, report the before and after for five metrics as a range, and close the packet with every generated artifact and every status row reconciled to that one observed state.

### Decisions

Frozen choices. Changing one is an amendment.

| ID | Decision |
|----|----------|
| D1 | The run brief is `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/brief-1.txt` byte for byte, the phase 1 T009 task at Level 3. No word changes on the final side. |
| D2 | M2 counts bare v2.2 hint occurrences plus v2.3 value token occurrences. The two patterns are disjoint and each unfilled instruction counts once. |
| D3 | Three final runs, serial, on cli-devin `swe-2-max`, compared with the 3 `swe-2-max` baseline runs. No run is dropped when it is bad. |
| D4 | The report folder is `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/benchmark/reports/$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline/`, dated by execution. |
| D5 | This phase is the only one allowed to edit the parent `spec.md`, the parent `goal.md` and the 041 phase map row for `006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination`. |
| D6 | A parent goal box is ticked only after its `C<n>` line reads PASS in `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt`. |
| D7 | Nothing is committed and nothing is pushed. Both stay with the operator. |

<!-- /ANCHOR:directive -->

- [x] The benchmark report folder holds `README.md` with ten numbered sections, `results.csv` with six runs, `source.md` that names this packet, and one new row in the reports index

### Deviations and findings

| Item | Note |
|------|------|
| Report folder name and the kebab checker | `check_authored_name_kebab.py` rejects the benchmark grammar `date--subject--variant`, because the checker allows a single hyphen between words. The benchmark skill requires the double hyphen, so the report uses the skill grammar and T008 checks it with its own pattern. |
| Report file name | `sk-create-benchmark` names `benchmark-report.md`. Every existing folder under `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/benchmark/reports/` holds `README.md`, and the index links to the folder. This phase writes `README.md` and keeps the ten-section contract. |
| Nested changelog file names | The generator prints `changelog-054-root.md` and `changelog-054-009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout.md` on this tree. The workflow reference describes the shape more loosely, so the generator output wins and the literal names in this phase use it. |
| Three runs cannot support a significance claim | The report prints a range and says so. A reader who needs an effect size needs more runs. |
| Bracket scan false positives | The bracket-hint scan matches the status format token in `plan.md` and the folder-name regex in `tasks.md`. Both sit inside fenced code blocks and are intentional literals, not unfilled hints. |
| Takeover on 2026-10-24 | The parent moved every remaining run to cli-codex gpt-6-luna max fast and cli-devin swe-2-max, and every review to swe-2-max (parent D4, D7, D8). | Run protocol on 2026-10-25 | The runs mirror the script that produced the SWE 2 Max baseline. Each dispatch uses the baseline brief-1.txt copied byte for byte (cmp), runs under a 2700 second watchdog on cli-devin swe-2-max with --permission-mode dangerous, and records its exit status. M5 is the dispatch end second minus the dispatch start second, as in the baseline, not the measurement time. M2 is the bare hint count (hints-l3.txt) plus the value token count. M4 is the status diff, and a change in the hash of git diff -- .skilled before and after a run also counts as an out-of-scope write. Each run's packet is copied to the scratchpad outside the worktree before it is removed. The session audit selects Devin sessions in the worktree between the start second and the end second plus 60, requires exactly one, and searches both tool_call_json and tool_call_update_json. A rate-limit or billing reply is not a run and is redone for the same run number. Extra per-run fields (exit status, bare and token split, audit) are in scratch/baseline/runs-meta.jsonl so baseline.json keeps the six keys per run. |

> row MUST name an ADR that exists in `decision-record.md`.

### Status values

| Value | Meaning |
|-------|---------|
| `Met` | Verified. The Verification cell names evidence that was actually observed. |
| `Unmet` | Not yet satisfied. Blocks closure. |
| `Waived` | Deliberately not pursued. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |
| `Superseded` | Replaced by a different criterion or decision. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |

### Waiver cell

Write `-` when the row is `Met` or `Unmet`. Write `ADR-NNN` when the row is
`Waived` or `Superseded`, naming a decision record that exists in
`decision-record.md`. A waiver naming an ADR that is not there fails validation:
the point of a waiver is that someone recorded the reasoning, so an unbacked
waiver is treated as an unmet criterion rather than as a pass.
<!-- /ANCHOR:criteria -->

All fifteen rows are Met. The seven parent proofs in `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt` read PASS, and criterion 5 passed on the second fresh set after three failed final sets that stay on record.

# Implementation Summary

<!-- SPECKIT_LEVEL: 3 -->
<!-- HVR_REFERENCE: .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-with-human-voice/references/hvr-rules.md -->

<!-- ANCHOR:what-built -->
## What Was Built

The packet now ends with a measured before and after, and the measurement found one failure. Three final runs of the frozen Level 3 authoring task are recorded beside the SWE 2 Max baseline. Every final run passed strict validation, the final maximum of `leftover_hints` is 0 against a baseline maximum of 1, and no run wrote outside its track. Parent criterion 5 is FAIL on the frozen metric, because run 3 left 15 anchors in `tasks.md` where the baseline expects 21, so `missing_anchors` has a final maximum of 6 against 0.

The six anchors run 3 deleted are the optional Level 3 verification sections: `arch-verify`, `perf-verify`, `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `docs-verify` and `sign-off`. A diagnostic, chosen after the result and not a verdict, scores all six runs against the anchors the contract requires (`template-structure.js contract 3 tasks.md` lists 15 required and these 6 optional), and every run misses none. Three of the deletions went against the template's own fill notes, because the packet records ADR-001 (`plan.md` line 239 of run 3, kept as `scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/plan.txt`), states NFR-P01 (`spec.md` line 133 of run 3, kept as `scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/spec.txt`) and holds paired documents. The run's reply says "L3+ optional sections dropped (this is Level 3, not 3+)", so it read the `## L3+:` heading prefix as Level 3+ only. Run 3 also took 1822 seconds against 437 and 429, and the cause is unknown. The run log is `scratch/baseline/runs.jsonl`, the extras are in `runs-meta.jsonl` and the delta is `scratch/baseline/delta.json`.

### Third amendment: label fix and second final set

The fill note under each of the six `## L3+:` headings of the `tasks.md` template now opens with 'L3+ means Level 3 and above, so this section stays at Level 3.' The phase 004 plan rows, the golden snapshot and the guards match it, and the full suite passed again with the same counts. The operator ruled out the swe-2 models, so a new DeepSeek baseline of three runs at the packet start commit was taken by a separate agent, and three final runs ran on cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` on the amended tree. Strict validation passed in all three and leftover hints were 0 in all three, but run 1 wrote a `tasks.md` without `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify` and `sign-off`, so `missing_anchors` has a final maximum of 3 against a baseline maximum of 0 and criterion 5 fails again. Run 2 is contaminated, because its session read the frozen vocabulary files, and it is kept in the numbers and labelled. The first final set stays on record as history, measured on the earlier tree on swe-2-max.

### New anchor rule and fresh runs

The operator then chose "New rule, fresh runs". A rule script, `scratch/tools/justified-anchors.sh`, was committed with its scores on the twelve earlier packets before any fresh run: a frozen anchor missing from a packet counts unless it is an optional Level 3 `tasks.md` anchor whose fill-note condition holds. The first swe-2-max final set scores 0, 0 and 3 under it and the second DeepSeek set scores 0, 0 and 0. Three fresh runs then ran on cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` in a disposable worktree with this packet's folder removed from its working tree. All three passed strict validation with no leftover hints, but run 1 deleted the whole required `fix-completeness` section, so counted `missing_anchors` is 1, 0 and 0 against a baseline maximum of 0 and criterion 5 fails for the third time. Run 3's deleted sections (`deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `sign-off`) are excused by the rule.

### Fix-completeness note fixed and a second fresh set

The cause of the third failure was found and fixed at the source: strict validation does not check that required anchors are present, and the required `fix-completeness` fill note said 'Delete the rows that do not apply', which the failing run read as permission to delete the section. The note now says to keep the section and delete only rows. The anchor rule and criterion 5 did not change. After the full suite passed again with the same counts, a second fresh set ran on cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` in a new disposable worktree with this packet's folder removed. All three runs passed strict validation, leftover hints were 0, 1 and 0 against a baseline maximum of 1, counted `missing_anchors` was 0, 0 and 0 against 0, and nothing was written outside the track. **Criterion 5 passes on this fourth final set.** The three earlier sets failed and stay on record, each labelled with its model, tree and rule. The PASS followed a fix made after a failure was understood, so it is not a first-try result, and three runs on one model give a range and not an effect size.

### Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

The closeout also published the benchmark report with verdict FAIL, wrote the global changelog `v2.9.0.0` and bumped the version carrier, wrote the nested changelogs for phases 001 to 003 and this phase, regenerated the trigger index, confirmed the track roots and reconciled the parent phase map and goal log. `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt` holds one line for each parent criterion: six PASS lines and one FAIL line, `C5 FAIL`.

| File | Action | Purpose |

<!-- ANCHOR:how-delivered -->
## How It Was Delivered

One Claude Code worker ran the phase and spawned no subagent. It ran three serial cli-devin swe-2-max dispatches of the frozen Level 3 brief and audited each run's Devin session. It measured each run with the five commands recorded in `scratch/tools/run-final.sh`, wrote every number in the report from `delta.json` and the run files, and proved each parent criterion before ticking any box. The census for criterion 3 was rerun over the frozen folder list, which excludes this packet. One read-only frontier review on cli-codex gpt-6-luna read the closeout diff, and each finding is recorded in `goal.md` section 4. The operator moved the review from the cli-devin swe-2-max that parent D4 names.

### Operator handoff

- The original close-out made one local commit (14cd9aabcf), and the reconcile and the amendments added local commits after it (fab90dccbd, 23657e227e, e13115c7da and the closing commit of the fresh set). Nothing was pushed.
- Criterion 5 passed on the second fresh set, so the phase and the parent read Complete. Reviewing the local commits and the push are the operator's steps.
- The disposable worktrees `223-deepseek-final` and `224-deepseek-final2` and their branches are left in place for the operator to remove.
- No generated artifact needs a hand edit before review.
<!-- /ANCHOR:how-delivered -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:decisions -->
## Key Decisions

| Decision | Why |
|----------|-----|
| Keep the frozen metric and the FAIL, and add a labelled diagnostic | The operator chose "Option 3 + diagnostic". Changing the metric after the result would hide the finding |
| Count bare hints plus value tokens for M2 | The two patterns are disjoint, so each unfilled instruction counts once on both sides |
| Use Level 3 for the final runs | The SWE 2 Max baseline is Level 3 |
| Prove parent criterion 3 on the frozen folder list | A live walk would compare different populations |
| Keep the first final set as history and label the second set as the final one | The sets differ in model and tree, so each is reported with its own baseline |
| Score every earlier set under the new rule before any fresh run | The operator's row claims two scores, and the script had to reproduce them first |
| Keep a contaminated run in the numbers | Dropping it would hide what the session read, and the verdict does not depend on it |
| Mark the phase and the parent Complete only after criterion 5 passed | The validator reads a Complete status with an unmet acceptance row as a failed closure, and every row is now Met |
<!-- /ANCHOR:decisions -->

<!-- ANCHOR:limitations -->
## Known Limitations

1. **Criterion 5 failed three times before it passed.** `missing_anchors` had a final maximum of 6, 3 and (counted under the new rule) 1 against 0 on the first three final sets, and the fourth set passed. The result is a range over three runs on one model after a targeted template fix.
2. **The fix was made after the failure.** The `fix-completeness` note was corrected after a run deleted that section, and the next set passed. The report states this plainly, and a larger run count and a second model would be needed to separate a fix from variation.
3. **Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim.** One run produced the whole failure, so the sample cannot separate the template change from run-to-run variation.
4. **The fixture is one small feature and four documents.** A different feature shape or a Level 1 packet is not measured.
5. **Acceptance rows were Unmet until criterion 5 passed.** AC-010, AC-011 and AC-015 needed nine Complete map rows, seven PASS lines and a Complete status, and they are Met now.
6. **The validator does not enforce required anchors.** Strict validation checks the form of anchors and not that each required `tasks.md` anchor is present, so a packet without the required `fix-completeness` section passed. The template note was corrected to say to keep the section, and the validator rule is an open follow-up that is not fixed here.
<!-- /ANCHOR:limitations -->

# Tasks: Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

<!-- ANCHOR:notation -->
## Task Notation

| Prefix | Meaning |
|--------|---------|
| `[ ]` | Pending |
| `[x]` | Completed |
| `[P]` | Parallelizable |
| `[B]` | Blocked |

**Task Format**: `T### [P?] Description (file path)`, then `Verify:` with the command whose output proves the task.

Shell variables used below, set once by the conductor:
- `WORKTREE`: the build worktree path recorded in `../goal.md` section 4.
- `PKT`: `$WORKTREE/specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring`
- `PHASE`: `$PKT/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout`
- `SC`: `$PHASE/scratch`
- `S`: `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit`
- `P1`: `$PKT/001-template-drift-census-and-baseline`
- `BL`: `$P1/scratch/baseline-swe-l3`, the Level 3 cli-devin swe-2-max baseline.
- `P3`: `$PKT/003-versioned-placeholder-gate`
- `START`: the commit hash in `$SC/baseline/start-commit.txt`, recorded by T003.

Every command runs from `$WORKTREE` unless the task says otherwise. Numbers printed in a Verify line that depend on the run are written as a rule, not as a fixed value. The baseline side is read from `$BL`: `baseline.json` for the three runs, `brief-1.txt` for the frozen brief, `hints-l3.txt` for the bare hint list and `anchors-l3.txt` for the required anchors.
<!-- /ANCHOR:notation -->

<!-- ANCHOR:phase-2 -->
## Phase 2: Implementation

- [x] T004 Build the final vocabulary and anchor list (`scratch/baseline/hints-v2.3.txt`, `scratch/baseline/anchors-v2.3.txt`). Run the block below. It scaffolds one Level 3 packet, the level the runs use, extracts the distinct value tokens and the anchor count of each of the four documents, then deletes the throwaway track. Both files record the final templates. M3 in T006 still measures against `$BL/anchors-l3.txt`, so both sides share one anchor definition. Verify: `wc -l < "$SC/baseline/hints-v2.3.txt"` prints a number above 50, `cat "$SC/baseline/anchors-v2.3.txt"` prints four lines, and `test ! -e specs/zz-bench-hints && echo TRACK_GONE` prints `TRACK_GONE`. Record both counts in `goal.md` section 4.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 3 --track zz-bench-hints --short-name recommend-level-quiet-flag "Add a quiet flag to recommend-level.sh"
  P=specs/zz-bench-hints/001-recommend-level-quiet-flag
  rg -o --no-filename '\[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: [^]]*\]' $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md $P/acceptance-criteria.md | sort -u > "$SC/baseline/hints-v2.3.txt"
  for f in spec plan tasks acceptance-criteria; do echo "$f $(rg -c '<!-- ANCHOR:' $P/$f.md)"; done > "$SC/baseline/anchors-v2.3.txt"
  rm -r specs/zz-bench-hints
  ```

- [x] T005 Run the final baseline three times, serially (`scratch/runs/T005-1.txt` to `T005-3.txt`, `scratch/baseline/runs.jsonl`). For each run `N` in 1, 2, 3, in order:
  1. Save `git status --porcelain` to `$SC/baseline/status-before-$N.txt`.
  2. Save `date +%s` to `$SC/runs/T005-$N.t0` before the dispatch.
  3. Run the dispatch block below. It copies the frozen brief and runs it on cli-devin swe-2-max, the benchmark subject. Run nothing else in the worktree while the run is live, because any write there counts as the subject's out-of-scope write.
  4. Run the audit block below. Each of its three lines must end in `0`. A higher count means the subject read baseline evidence, an earlier run or a conductor file: record the run as contaminated in `goal.md` section 4, keep it in `runs.jsonl`, and stop the phase for the operator.
  5. Run T006 for that run before starting the next one.

  Verify: `for n in 1 2 3; do test -s "$SC/runs/T005-$n.txt" && test -s "$SC/runs/T005-$n.t0" && test -s "$SC/runs/T005-$n.t1" && test -s "$SC/baseline/status-before-$n.txt" && echo "run-$n recorded"; done` prints `run-1 recorded`, `run-2 recorded` and `run-3 recorded`, and the frozen-text check prints `TASK_FROZEN`.

  Dispatch block for run `N`. The brief is the Level 3 baseline brief with no word changed:

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  cp "$BL/brief-1.txt" "$SC/briefs/T005-$N.txt"
  AI_SESSION_CHILD=1 SYSTEM_SPEC_GATE_ENFORCE=0 devin -p --model swe-2-max --permission-mode dangerous -- "$(cat "$SC/briefs/T005-$N.txt")" </dev/null > "$SC/runs/T005-$N.txt" 2> "$SC/runs/T005-$N.err"
  date +%s > "$SC/runs/T005-$N.t1"
  ```

  Audit block for run `N`. It finds the run's Devin session by working directory and start time and counts tool calls that name a forbidden path:

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  DB=~/.local/share/devin/cli/sessions.db
  SID=$(sqlite3 -readonly "$DB" "select id from sessions where working_directory='$WORKTREE' and created_at >= $(cat "$SC/runs/T005-$N.t0") order by created_at limit 1;")
  echo "$SID" > "$SC/runs/T005-$N.session"
  sqlite3 -readonly "$DB" "select tool_call_json from tool_call_state where session_id='$SID';" > "$SC/runs/T005-$N.calls"
  for p in baseline-swe 009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch /private/tmp; do echo "$p $(grep -o "$p" "$SC/runs/T005-$N.calls" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')"; done
  ```

  Frozen-text check, run after the three briefs exist:

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  CHANGED=0; for n in 1 2 3; do cmp -s "$BL/brief-1.txt" "$SC/briefs/T005-$n.txt" || { echo "BRIEF_CHANGED $n"; CHANGED=1; }; done; [ "$CHANGED" = 0 ] && echo TASK_FROZEN
  ```

- [x] T006 Measure each final run, then delete its track (part of `scratch/baseline/baseline.json`). Run the block below after run `N` stops, with `N` set to the run number, before the next dispatch. Verify: after the third run, `jq '.runs | length' "$SC/baseline/baseline.json"` prints `3`, `jq '.runs[] | keys | length' "$SC/baseline/baseline.json"` prints `6` three times, and `test ! -e specs/zz-bench-authoring && echo TRACK_GONE` prints `TRACK_GONE`.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  P=specs/zz-bench-authoring/001-recommend-level-quiet-flag
  HL3="$BL/hints-l3.txt"
  AL3="$BL/anchors-l3.txt"
  T0=$(cat "$SC/runs/T005-$N.t0")
  M1=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep -c 'RESULT: PASSED')
  M2=$( { grep -F -o -f "$HL3" $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md $P/acceptance-criteria.md 2>/dev/null; rg -o --no-filename '\[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: [^]]*\]' $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md $P/acceptance-criteria.md 2>/dev/null; } | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  M3=0; while read f n; do have=$(rg -c '<!-- ANCHOR:' $P/$f.md 2>/dev/null || echo 0); [ "$have" -lt "$n" ] && M3=$((M3 + n - have)); done < "$AL3"
  git status --porcelain > "$SC/baseline/status-after-$N.txt"
  M4=$(diff "$SC/baseline/status-before-$N.txt" "$SC/baseline/status-after-$N.txt" | grep '^>' | grep -v 'zz-bench-authoring' | grep -v '054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  M5=$(( $(date +%s) - T0 ))
  echo "{\"run\":$N,\"strict_passed\":$M1,\"leftover_hints\":$M2,\"missing_anchors\":$M3,\"out_of_scope_writes\":$M4,\"seconds\":$M5}" >> "$SC/baseline/runs.jsonl"
  jq -s '{runs: .}' "$SC/baseline/runs.jsonl" > "$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
  rm -r specs/zz-bench-authoring
  ```

  When `M4` is above 0, restore each listed path with `git restore --source="$START" -- <path>` before the next run and record the paths in `goal.md` section 4. When a run did not create the packet folder, record the run output in `goal.md` section 4, re-dispatch once, and keep the failed run id in the log.

- [x] T007 Write the delta for the five metrics (`scratch/baseline/delta.json`, `scratch/baseline/delta.md`). Run the block below. Verify: `jq '.metrics | length' "$SC/baseline/delta.json"` prints `5`, `grep -cE '^\| (strict_passed|leftover_hints|missing_anchors|out_of_scope_writes|seconds) \|' "$SC/baseline/delta.md"` prints `5`, and the last command prints `true`. When it prints `false`, write `C5 FAILED: a final run failed strict validation or is worse than the baseline's worst on leftover_hints, missing_anchors or out_of_scope_writes` in `goal.md` section 4, run T008 only, then write `STOP` in `goal.md` section 4 and stop the closeout. Do not tick the parent criterion and do not publish a changelog.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  B="$BL/baseline.json"
  F="$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
  jq -n --slurpfile b "$B" --slurpfile f "$F" '
    def stat($runs; $k):
      {min: ($runs | map(.[$k]) | min),
       max: ($runs | map(.[$k]) | max),
       mean: (($runs | map(.[$k]) | add) / ($runs | length))};
    ["strict_passed","leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes","seconds"] as $keys
    | {metrics: ($keys | map(. as $k | {metric: $k, baseline: stat($b[0].runs; $k), final: stat($f[0].runs; $k)}))}
    | .metrics |= map(. + {delta_mean: (.final.mean - .baseline.mean)})
  ' > "$SC/baseline/delta.json"
  jq -r '
    def one: (.*10 | round) / 10;
    "# Authoring delta\n",
    "- Baseline: three Level 3 runs of the frozen brief on cli-devin swe-2-max, recorded in `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/baseline.json`.",
    "- Final: three runs of the same task, recorded in `scratch/baseline/baseline.json`.",
    "- Each cell is the minimum, the maximum and the mean over three runs.",
    "- Three runs cannot support a significance claim. The table reports a range, not an effect size.\n",
    "| Metric | Baseline min | Baseline max | Baseline mean | Final min | Final max | Final mean | Delta of means |",
    "|--------|--------------|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|",
    (.metrics[] | "| \(.metric) | \(.baseline.min) | \(.baseline.max) | \(.baseline.mean | one) | \(.final.min) | \(.final.max) | \(.final.mean | one) | \(.delta_mean | one) |")
  ' "$SC/baseline/delta.json" > "$SC/baseline/delta.md"
  jq -e 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max))' "$SC/baseline/delta.json"
  ```

- [x] T008 Write the benchmark report and its index row (`$S/benchmark/reports/$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline/`, `$S/benchmark/reports/README.md`). Read `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/spec.md`, `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/plan.md` and `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/implementation-summary.md` for the report shape phase 8 accepted, and read `.skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-benchmark/SKILL.md` sections 3 and 5 for the required package shape and the report contract, before composing the report. Run the block below, which writes `results.csv`, `README.md` and `source.md` from `delta.json` and inserts one index row. Verify: `awk 'NR>1' "$REPORT/results.csv" | wc -l` prints `6`, `grep -c '^## [0-9]' "$REPORT/README.md"` prints `10`, `grep -c 'model-eval--authoring-baseline' "$S/benchmark/reports/README.md"` prints `1`, and the script prints `NAME_OK`.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  DATE=$(date +%F)
  NAME="$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline"
  REPORT="$S/benchmark/reports/$NAME"
  DELTA="$SC/baseline/delta.json"
  B="$BL/baseline.json"
  F="$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
  echo "$NAME" | grep -qE '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}--[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*--[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$' && echo NAME_OK
  mkdir -p "$REPORT"
  {
    echo 'side,run,strict_passed,leftover_hints,missing_anchors,out_of_scope_writes,seconds'
    jq -r '.runs[] | "baseline,\(.run),\(.strict_passed),\(.leftover_hints),\(.missing_anchors),\(.out_of_scope_writes),\(.seconds)"' "$B"
    jq -r '.runs[] | "final,\(.run),\(.strict_passed),\(.leftover_hints),\(.missing_anchors),\(.out_of_scope_writes),\(.seconds)"' "$F"
  } > "$REPORT/results.csv"
  val() { jq -r --arg m "$1" --arg s "$2" --arg k "$3" '.metrics[] | select(.metric==$m) | .[$s][$k]' "$DELTA"; }
  L_B_MIN=$(val leftover_hints baseline min); L_B_MAX=$(val leftover_hints baseline max)
  L_F_MIN=$(val leftover_hints final min);   L_F_MAX=$(val leftover_hints final max)
  VERDICT=$(jq -r 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max)) | if . then "PASS" else "FAIL" end' "$DELTA")
  {
    printf '# %s\n\n' "$NAME"
    printf '> system-spec-kit · model-eval · authoring-baseline · cli-devin swe-2-max\n\n---\n\n'
    printf '## 1. HEADLINE / OVERVIEW\n\n'
    printf '**Verdict: %s**\n\n' "$VERDICT"
    printf 'Three baseline runs and three final runs of one frozen Level 3 authoring brief. Leftover hints run from %s to %s on the baseline side and from %s to %s on the final side. The verdict is PASS when every final run passes strict validation and none is worse than the worst baseline run on leftover hints, missing anchors or out-of-scope writes.\n\n' "$L_B_MIN" "$L_B_MAX" "$L_F_MIN" "$L_F_MAX"
    printf '## 2. AGGREGATE RESULTS\n\n'
    echo '| Metric | Baseline min | Baseline max | Baseline mean | Final min | Final max | Final mean | Delta of means |'
    echo '|--------|--------------|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|'
    jq -r 'def one: (.*10 | round) / 10; .metrics[] | "| \(.metric) | \(.baseline.min) | \(.baseline.max) | \(.baseline.mean | one) | \(.final.min) | \(.final.max) | \(.final.mean | one) | \(.delta_mean | one) |"' "$DELTA"
    printf '\n## 3. METHODOLOGY\n\n'
    cat <<'MD' | sed 's/^  //'
  Fixture: one Level 3 packet for a `--quiet` flag on `recommend-level.sh`. The brief is frozen and copied from the phase 1 baseline with no word changed. Each side is three serial runs by cli-devin `swe-2-max`, and each run's Devin session is audited for reads of baseline evidence. The measured documents are `spec.md`, `plan.md`, `tasks.md` and `acceptance-criteria.md`. The throwaway track is deleted after each measurement.

  ## 4. PER-CANDIDATE PROFILES

  | Side | Tree | What the worker saw |
  |---|---|---|
  | baseline | Before the packet changes | v2.2 templates, bare bracketed hints, the hand-written examples |
  | final | After phases 1 to 8 | v2.3 value tokens, section fill guidance, the authoring card, the guards |

  ## 5. PROCESS NOTES

  The baseline side ran in phase 1 on templates identical to the start commit and was not rerun. The final side ran after every phase closed, so the worker saw the tree the packet ships. A run that left its track behind was restored before the next dispatch.

  ## 6. FINDINGS

  The load-bearing numbers are the worst final run on leftover hints, missing anchors and out-of-scope writes, read against the worst baseline run. The baseline already reached 0 hints in one run, so the packet claims no regression rather than a lower minimum. A wide final range means the templates help some runs and not others.

  ## 7. CAVEATS

  Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim. The fixture is one small feature and four documents. The model is one model at one effort. A template change and a card change landed together, so this report cannot separate their effects.

  ## 8. RECOMMENDATIONS

  - **Apply now.** Keep the v2.3 token gate and the card. Both are measured on the same task.
  - **Validate first.** Rerun this report after the next template change with a new date and the same variant.
  - **Future.** Add a second feature shape if a future packet needs to know whether the gain holds outside one small feature.

  # v2.9.0.0, Templates a Weak Model Can Follow

  A weak model can now see what a template still needs, because every hint in the five core templates is a value token that fails validation once the packet leaves Draft. The four authoring intents also load a short card rather than the long guide, and the examples no longer teach the defects the old text carried.

  > Spec folder: `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring` (Level 2)

  &nbsp;

  ## What's New at a Glance

  - **An unfilled hint can no longer pass as finished.** A doc stamped v2.3 warns while the spec is Draft and fails strict validation at any other status.
  - **Every core section says how to fill it.** Each section carries a short instruction and a good and weak example.
  - **Authoring starts on one page.** The router loads a short card for the four authoring intents.
  - **The examples stop teaching the old defects.** Their descriptions, stale checklist lines and anchors now match the templates.
  - **Commands and agents name the new gate and the card.** A speckit or doctor run describes the same authoring path a worker follows.
  - **The playbook and catalog cover the new behavior.** A focused run report records each scenario result.
  - **The result is measured, not asserted.** Three runs of one frozen authoring task on each side give a range for five metrics.

  &nbsp;

  ## Upgrade

  No migration required. Existing packets keep their verdict, because the token gate applies only to docs stamped v2.3. Scaffold a new packet to get the new templates and the authoring card.

When a line reads FAIL, write it in `goal.md` section 4, leave the matching parent box unticked in T017 and do not claim the packet is closeable. **Partly met (record reconcile on 2026-10-25).** The verify line asks for seven PASS lines, but criteria.txt holds six PASS lines and the C5 FAIL line, which is the contingency this task describes. The task text only asks to record a FAIL and leave its parent box unticked, which was done.

  ```bash
  cd "$WORKTREE"
  C="$SC/closeout/criteria.txt"
  : > "$C"

  # C1: recursive strict on the packet.
  OUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PKT" --recursive --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1)
  if [ "$OUT" = "RESULT: PASSED" ]; then echo "C1 PASS $OUT" >> "$C"; else echo "C1 FAIL $OUT" >> "$C"; fi

  # C2: the token gate on a fresh v2.3 packet and on an old stamp.
  rm -rf specs/zz-bench-closeout
  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 1 --track zz-bench-closeout --short-name gate "Gate" > "$SC/closeout/c2-create.txt" 2>&1
  P=specs/zz-bench-closeout/001-gate
  D=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1)
  perl -pi -e 's/\| \*\*Status\*\* \| Draft \|/| **Status** | Complete |/' $P/spec.md
  K=$(env SPECKIT_RULES=PLACEHOLDER_FILLED bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep -c 'placeholder(s) found')
  perl -pi -e 's/\| \*\*Status\*\* \| Complete \|/| **Status** | Draft |/' $P/spec.md
  perl -pi -e 's/\| v2\.3 -->/| v2.2 -->/' $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md
  V=$(env SPECKIT_RULES=PLACEHOLDER_FILLED bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep -c 'placeholder(s) found')
  rm -rf specs/zz-bench-closeout
  if [ "$D" = "RESULT: PASSED" ] && [ "$K" -ge 1 ] && [ "$V" -ge 1 ]; then echo "C2 PASS draft=$D complete_errors=$K v22_errors=$V" >> "$C"; else echo "C2 FAIL draft=$D complete_errors=$K v22_errors=$V" >> "$C"; fi

  # C3: the frozen phase 3 census, rerun on the final tree.
  ORCH="$S/runtime/dist/lib/validation/orchestrator.js"
  for i in 0 1 2; do env -u SPECKIT_RULES node "$P3/scratch/tools/strict-census.mjs" "$ORCH" "$P3/scratch/census/shard-$i.txt" > "$SC/closeout/b-final-$i.tsv" 2> "$SC/closeout/b-final-$i.err" & done
  wait
  cat "$SC/closeout/b-final-"[012].tsv | LC_ALL=C sort > "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv"
  cut -f1,2,5 "$P3/scratch/census/b-before.tsv" > "$SC/closeout/b-before.cut"
  cut -f1,2,5 "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv" > "$SC/closeout/b-final.cut"
  DIFFROWS=$(diff "$SC/closeout/b-before.cut" "$SC/closeout/b-final.cut" | grep -c '^[<>]')
  BCOUNT=$(cut -f2 "$P3/scratch/census/b-before.tsv" | sort | uniq -c | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ' ')
  FCOUNT=$(cut -f2 "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv" | sort | uniq -c | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ' ')
  if [ "$DIFFROWS" = "0" ]; then echo "C3 PASS CENSUS_SAME gate B before[$BCOUNT] after[$FCOUNT]" >> "$C"; else echo "C3 FAIL diff_rows=$DIFFROWS before[$BCOUNT] after[$FCOUNT]" >> "$C"; fi

  # C4: the two guards.
  AOUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/check-example-drift.sh 2>&1); AR=$?
  A=$(printf '%s\n' "$AOUT" | tail -1)
  BOUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/check-card-sync.sh 2>&1); BR=$?
  B=$(printf '%s\n' "$BOUT" | tail -1)
  if [ "$A" = "EXAMPLE_DRIFT_OK" ] && [ "$B" = "CARD SYNC OK" ] && [ "$AR" = "0" ] && [ "$BR" = "0" ]; then echo "C4 PASS $A $B" >> "$C"; else echo "C4 FAIL $A rc=$AR $B rc=$BR" >> "$C"; fi

  # C5: the report covers five metrics on two sides of three runs, and no final run regressed against the worst baseline run.
  N=$(jq '.metrics | length' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
  BRUNS=$(jq '.runs | length' "$BL/baseline.json")
  FRUNS=$(jq '.runs | length' "$SC/baseline/baseline.json")
  LOW=$(jq -r '([.metrics[] | select(.metric=="leftover_hints")][0]) | "\(.baseline.max) -> \(.final.max)"' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
  OK=$(jq -r 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max)) | if . then "yes" else "no" end' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
  if [ "$N" = "5" ] && [ "$BRUNS" = "3" ] && [ "$FRUNS" = "3" ] && [ "$OK" = "yes" ]; then echo "C5 PASS metrics=$N runs=$BRUNS+$FRUNS leftover_max=$LOW" >> "$C"; else echo "C5 FAIL metrics=$N runs=$BRUNS+$FRUNS leftover_max=$LOW" >> "$C"; fi

  # C6: every playbook scenario this packet added has a PASS or an escalated FAIL.
  REPORTS="$S/benchmark/reports"
  REPORT=$(for d in "$REPORTS"/*--manual-testing-playbook--*/; do test -s "$d/source.md" && rg -q '054-weak-model-spec-authoring' "$d/source.md" && echo "$d"; done | sort | tail -1)
  ADDED=$({ git diff --name-only "$START" -- "$S/manual-testing-playbook/"; git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$S/manual-testing-playbook/"; } | sort -u | rg '\.md$' | rg -v 'manual-testing-playbook\.md$')
  MISS=0; LINES=""
  if [ -z "$REPORT" ]; then echo "C6 FAIL MISSING-008-REPORT" >> "$C"; elif [ -z "$ADDED" ]; then echo "C6 FAIL NO_SCENARIO_FILES" >> "$C"; else
    for f in $ADDED; do
      SLUG=$(basename "$(dirname "$f")")-$(basename "$f" .md)
      NUM=$(rg -m1 -o '^title: "([0-9]+)' -r '$1' "$f")
      V=$(awk -F, -v id="$SLUG" -v num="$NUM" 'NR==1{for(i=1;i<=NF;i++){if($i=="scenario_id")s=i; if($i=="verdict")v=i}; next} ($s==id || $s==num){print $v}' "$REPORT/results.csv" | head -1)
      if [ "$V" != "PASS" ]; then
        if rg -q 'FAIL' "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md" && { rg -q --fixed-strings "$SLUG" "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md" || rg -q --fixed-strings "$NUM" "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md"; }; then V="FAIL-LOGGED"; else MISS=$((MISS+1)); fi
      fi
      LINES="$LINES $SLUG=$V"
    done
    if [ "$MISS" = "0" ]; then echo "C6 PASS$LINES" >> "$C"; else echo "C6 FAIL missing=$MISS$LINES" >> "$C"; fi
  fi

  # C7: swarm commits stay local and nothing is pushed.
  HEAD=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
  COUNT=$(git log --oneline "$START..HEAD" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  REMOTE=$(git branch -r --contains HEAD | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  if [ "$COUNT" = "0" ] || [ "$REMOTE" = "0" ]; then echo "C7 PASS head=$HEAD commits_since_start=$COUNT remote_branches_with_head=$REMOTE" >> "$C"; else echo "C7 FAIL head=$HEAD start=$START commits_since_start=$COUNT remote_branches_with_head=$REMOTE" >> "$C"; fi

  cat "$C"
  PASSES=$(grep -c '^C[1-7] PASS ' "$C")
  N="$PASSES" perl -0pi -e 's{^(\| BASE_REF \|.*\n)}{$1 . "| Parent criteria | Done | $ENV{N} PASS lines in scratch/closeout/criteria.txt |\n"}me unless /\| Parent criteria \|/' "$PKT/goal.md"
  echo "CRITERIA_LOGGED passes=$PASSES"
  ```

- [x] T016 Frontier review of the closeout diff (`scratch/review/T016.txt`). Read `.skilled/skills/cli-external-orchestration/cli-codex/SKILL.md` first. Save the block below as `$SC/review/T016-brief.txt`, then run the command. Verify: the file exists, the format check below prints `T016_FORMAT ... bad=0` with exactly one of `findings` or `no_findings` non-zero. Apply each finding or record it in `goal.md` section 4 as `T016-APPLIED <finding>` or `T016-RECORDED <finding> <reason>`, and check `test "$(grep -cE '^(P0|P1|P2) ' "$SC/review/T016.txt")" -eq "$(grep -cE '^T016-(APPLIED|RECORDED) ' "$PHASE/goal.md")" && echo T016_COUNTS_MATCH` prints `T016_COUNTS_MATCH`. **Ran with adapted file names (record reconcile on 2026-10-25).** The reviewer ran on cli-codex gpt-6-luna as written, from scratch/briefs/T016.txt, with the raw reply in scratch/review/T016.last.txt. scratch/review/T016.txt holds the extracted P1 and P2 lines, and scratch/review/T016.check.txt shows T016_FORMAT findings=3 bad=0 and T016_COUNTS_MATCH.   cd "$WORKTREE"

  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PKT/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[x\]'
  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PKT/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[ \]' || true
  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PHASE/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[x\]'

<!-- SPECKIT_TEMPLATE_SOURCE: spec-core + level2-verify + level3-arch | v2.2 -->
# Feature Specification: Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

<!-- SPECKIT_LEVEL: 3 -->


---

## EXECUTIVE SUMMARY

Phase 1 first measured a DeepSeek V4.1 Flash worker on a Level 2 packet, and the parent later replaced that with a Level 3 baseline on cli-devin swe-2-max, taken before any template changed. This phase measures the same Level 3 task the same way on the final tree and reports the difference for five metrics, each as a minimum and a maximum over three runs. It then publishes the benchmark report, writes the changelogs, regenerates the trigger index, refreshes the track roots and closes the packet against the seven criteria in `../goal.md` section 3.

**Key Decisions**: the frozen task text is copied byte for byte from phase 1, and leftover hints are counted as bare v2.2 hints plus v2.3 value tokens so the two sides share one definition.

**Critical Dependencies**: phases 1 to 8 are closed, the write-recipe child is closed and the launch preconditions in `../spec.md` section 3 are met and recorded in `../goal.md` section 4.

---
<!-- ANCHOR:metadata -->
## 1. METADATA

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Level** | 3 |
| **Priority** | P0 |
| **Status** | Complete |
| **Created** | 2026-10-23 |
| **Branch** | `main` |
| **Parent Spec** | ../spec.md |
| **Phase** | 9 of 9 |

<!-- ANCHOR:phase-context -->
## Phase Context

This is **Phase 9** of the Make system-spec-kit templates and authoring docs followable by a DeepSeek V4.1 Flash agent swarm specification.

**Scope Boundary**: Edits land in this phase folder, in the benchmark report folder, in the two changelog locations, in the trigger index artifacts, in the `system-speckit` track metadata, in the parent `spec.md` and `goal.md`, and in one row of the `041-write-recipe-fixes` phase map. No template, validator, rule, command, agent or playbook file changes. This is the only phase allowed to edit the parent `spec.md`, the parent `goal.md` and the 041 phase map. The exact rows are the nine status rows of the parent phase map for phases 1 to 9, the seven completion checkboxes in parent `goal.md` section 3, the progress and log rows in parent `goal.md` section 4, and the row for `006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination` in the 041 phase map.

**Dependencies**:
- The launch preconditions in `../spec.md` section 3 are met and recorded in `../goal.md` section 4.
- Phases 1 to 8 are closed. Phase 1 leaves `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline/baseline.json` and `hints-v2.2.txt`. Phase 3 leaves the frozen folder list in `../003-versioned-placeholder-gate/scratch/census/`. Phase 4 leaves `check-example-drift.sh`. Phase 5 leaves `check-card-sync.sh`. Phase 8 leaves one playbook run report.
- The write-recipe child `specs/system-speckit/041-write-recipe-fixes/006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination/` is closed and passes strict validation.

**Deliverables**:
- `scratch/baseline/hints-v2.3.txt` and `scratch/baseline/anchors-v2.3.txt`: the final token vocabulary and anchor counts from a fresh Level 3 scaffold.
- `scratch/baseline/runs.jsonl` and `scratch/baseline/baseline.json`: three final runs, each with five metrics.
- `scratch/baseline/delta.json` and `scratch/baseline/delta.md`: the minimum, the maximum and the mean of each metric per side, with the delta.
- `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/benchmark/reports/$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline/`: `README.md`, `results.csv` and `source.md`, plus one new index row in the reports `README.md`.
- `.skilled/changelog/system-spec-kit/v2.9.0.0.md`: the global changelog entry.
- `../changelog/changelog-054-root.md` and `../changelog/changelog-054-009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout.md`: the packet-local changelogs.
- `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt`: one PASS line with its command output for each of the seven parent criteria.
- `scratch/review/T016.txt`: the frontier review reply, one line per finding or `NO FINDINGS`.

**Changelog**:
- This phase writes the matching file in `../changelog/` with the nested generator and the global component entry in `.skilled/changelog/system-spec-kit/`.
<!-- /ANCHOR:phase-context -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:problem -->
## 2. PROBLEM & PURPOSE

### Problem Statement
Phases 1 to 8 change the templates, the validator, the examples, the authoring card, the commands and the playbooks. Without a second measurement on the same task, the packet can only claim that the files changed. It cannot say whether a weak model leaves fewer unfilled hints, hits the required anchors more often or writes outside its track less often. The packet also cannot close while the generated index, the track roots, the changelogs and the parent maps still describe a planned state.

### Purpose
The closing report states the before and after per metric as a range, and the packet closes with every generated artifact and every status row reconciled to one observed state.
<!-- /ANCHOR:problem -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:scope -->
## 3. SCOPE

### In Scope
- Three serial final runs of the frozen authoring task from `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/tasks.md` T009, with no word changed.
- The five metrics M1 to M5, measured by the same commands on both sides, with M2 extended to count v2.3 value tokens.
- A delta report with the minimum, the maximum and the mean of each metric per side, and an explicit statement that three runs cannot support a significance claim.
- One benchmark report folder that follows the run folder grammar and the ten-section report contract.
- A global changelog entry and two packet-local changelog files.
- One trigger index regeneration and one track root refresh.
- Reconciliation of the parent phase map, the parent goal log, the 041 phase map row and the completion metadata of this phase.
- One proof line per parent completion criterion, written before any parent goal box is ticked.
- One read-only frontier review of the closeout diff.

### Out of Scope
- Any template, validator, rule, command, agent or playbook edit. Phases 2 to 8 own those.
- Any change to the frozen task text, the four measured documents or the metric definitions. A change makes the delta meaningless.
- A significance claim. Three runs per side cannot support one.
- A push. This phase ends in one local commit on the worktree branch and nothing is pushed.
- Fixing another phase folder. A child that fails strict validation is named and the closeout stops, because another phase owns its docs.

<!-- ANCHOR:risks -->
## 6. RISKS & DEPENDENCIES

| Type | Item | Impact | Mitigation |
|------|------|--------|------------|
| Dependency | Phases 1 to 8 closed | There is no final tree to measure and no report from phase 8 | `tasks.md` T002 checks nine closeable statements and stops the phase when one is missing |
| Dependency | LLM Gateway provider for `llmgateway/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017` | No final run can start | T001 checks `opencode.json` before the first dispatch and records the error when it fails |
| Dependency | Phase 8 playbook report | Parent criterion 6 cannot be proven | T015 names the missing report and leaves the criterion unticked |
| Risk | The three final runs vary so much that the delta is noise | The report overclaims | The report prints the range and states that three runs cannot support a significance claim |
| Risk | A final run writes outside its throwaway track | The tree is dirty and the next phase inherits it | M4 counts those writes and the conductor restores each path before the next run |
| Risk | The report folder name uses the authoring date | The index row misdates the run | T008 takes the date from `date +%F` at execution time and validates the name with the kebab checker |
| Risk | A phase 1 to 8 document fails recursive strict | The packet cannot close | T017 names the failing child and stops. This phase does not edit another phase folder |
<!-- /ANCHOR:risks -->

---


## 7. NON-FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS

### Performance
- **NFR-P01**: Each final run is one Task call. Three runs run serially. A run that fails is recorded and dispatched once more.

### Security
- **NFR-S01**: Every brief carries no credential, token or key. Workers inherit the session model and its provider login.

### Reliability
- **NFR-R01**: Every number in the report is produced by a command in `tasks.md`, so a second conductor on the same tree gets the same numbers from the same runs.

---

## 8. EDGE CASES

### Data Boundaries
- Empty input: a final run that leaves a document empty still counts as a run. M2 and M3 record the result. The report does not drop the run.
- Maximum length: a metric value that is not an integer is written in `delta.md` to one decimal place and never rounded in `delta.json`.

### Error Scenarios
- External service failure: a provider refusal stops the dispatch. The conductor records the error, retries once after 10 minutes and then stops the phase with the failed run named.
- Network timeout: handled as in NFR-P01.
- Missing phase 8 report: T015 prints `C6 MISSING-008-REPORT`, the parent criterion stays unticked and the closeout stops.

# Goal: Make system-spec-kit templates and authoring docs followable by a weak-model agent swarm

## 1. DURABLE DIRECTIVE

**Objective:** A weak-model swarm authors valid, filled spec packets from the system-spec-kit templates, proved by a before and after measured on DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, with no change to the verdict of any existing packet.

### Decisions

Frozen choices. Changing one is an amendment.

| ID | Decision |
|----|----------|
| D1 | Phases 001 to 009 plus one child under `041-write-recipe-fixes`. All logic lives in system-spec-kit, never sk-prompt. |
| D2 | The placeholder gate applies to new docs only, via the v2.3 template marker. No existing or external packet gets a new failure. |
| D3 | At most 3 parallel lanes, never two on a file. |
| D4 | Every phase closes on scripted gates plus one read-only review (cli-devin, then cli-codex luna). |
| D5 | Phase 008 runs the playbooks in a disposable sk-git worktree. |
| D6 | Every phase is Level 3 with its own goal, bound below. |
| D7 | A Claude Code session conducts. Sonnet 5.5 high subagents execute and verify, plus cli-codex luna and DeepSeek V4.1 Flash max as needed. |
| D8 | The benchmark subject is cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max`. Phase 009 compares 3 final-tree runs with 3 start-commit runs. |
