# Feature Specification: Make system-spec-kit templates and authoring docs followable by a DeepSeek V4.1 Flash agent swarm

<!-- ANCHOR:metadata -->
## 1. METADATA

| Field | Value |
|-------|-------|
| **Level** | 2 |
| **Priority** | P1 |
| **Status** | Complete |
| **Created** | 2026-10-23 |
| **Branch** | `main` |
| **Parent Spec** | `../spec.md` |
| **Parent Packet** | system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring |
| **Predecessor** | None |
| **Successor** | None |
| **Handoff Criteria** | All nine phases are Complete, `validate.sh --recursive --strict` on this packet prints `RESULT: PASSED`, and phase 009 reports a measured before and after for the five authoring metrics |
<!-- /ANCHOR:metadata -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:problem -->
## 2. PROBLEM & PURPOSE

### Problem Statement
A weak-model worker (the benchmark subject is cli-devin `swe-2-max`, and single-change swarm lanes run on DeepSeek V4.1 Flash) that authors a spec packet from the system-spec-kit templates leaves scaffold hints in place and still passes `validate.sh --strict`. A fresh Level 2 scaffold holds 97 bracketed hint occurrences and validates clean. The hand-written examples the worker imitates carry leaked description text and stale lines. The authoring guidance is a 1,200-line template guide that a weak model cannot follow in one pass. Together these let a swarm produce packets that look finished and are not.

### Purpose
Make the templates, the examples, the validator and the authoring docs good enough that a weak-model swarm, measured on cli-devin `swe-2-max`, authors a valid, filled packet without a frontier model correcting it. Phase 001 measures the starting point and the last phase measures the result, so the claim rests on numbers.

> **Phase-parent note:** This spec.md is the ONLY authored document at the parent level. All detailed planning, task breakdowns, checklists, and decisions live in the child phase folders listed in the Phase Documentation Map below. This keeps the parent from drifting stale as phases execute and pivot.
<!-- /ANCHOR:problem -->

---

<!-- ANCHOR:scope -->
## 3. SCOPE

### In Scope
- Repair the known template, example and reference defects, found by a census and fixed by exact text swap.
- Gate unfilled placeholders on new docs only, through a template version marker.
- Add a one-line fill instruction with a good and a weak example to each core template section.
- Add a short authoring card and route the skill router to it.
- Check and update the speckit and doctor commands and the doc-authoring agents such as markdown.
- Align the playbook, catalog and READMEs of system-spec-kit, then run the playbooks in a disposable worktree.
- Measure the authoring baseline before any change and again at the end.
- One write-recipe fix, tracked as a child of the earlier write-recipe series.

### Out of Scope
- Any change to `sk-prompt`. All logic lives in system-spec-kit.
- Any change that alters the verdict for an existing packet, because external-repo users would break on `/doctor:update`.
- Measuring models other than cli-devin swe-2-max for the benchmark.
- Pushing. Each phase ends in one local commit on the worktree branch and nothing is pushed.

### Files to Change

| File Path | Change Type | Phase | Description |
|-----------|-------------|-------|-------------|
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/templates/examples/**` | Modify | 002, 004 | Example descriptions, stale lines, skeleton alignment |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/references/**` | Modify | 002, 005, 006 | Style guide, checklist, template guide, new card |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/templates/core/*.tmpl` | Modify | 003, 004, 005 | Token form, v2.3 marker, fill guidance, task `Verify:` slot |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/rules/check-placeholders.sh` and its TypeScript mirror | Modify | 003 | Versioned placeholder gate |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/SKILL.md`, `leaf-manifest.json`, `leaf-aliases.json` | Modify | 005 | Router loads the card |
| `.skilled/commands/speckit/`, `.skilled/commands/doctor/` speckit entries, `.skilled/agents/markdown.md` and other doc-authoring agents | Modify | 006 | Commands and agents match the new templates, gate and card |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/` guard scripts | Create | 004, 005 | Example drift guard and card sync guard |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/manual-testing-playbook/`, `feature-catalog/`, READMEs | Modify | 007 | Scenarios and docs for the new behavior |
| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/benchmark/reports/` | Create | 008, 009 | Playbook run report and benchmark report |
| `specs/system-speckit/041-write-recipe-fixes/006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination/` | Create | 041-006 | Write-recipe move destination fix |

### Launch preconditions

The operator meets all four before the first phase runs. The conductor records each as met in `goal.md` section 4 and stops when one is not.

1. **Workspace.** The operator chooses A) a git worktree or B) the current branch, following `sk-git`. The conductor never chooses. The chosen path becomes `WORKTREE` in `goal.md` section 4.
2. **Provider login.** `devin auth status` reports a Devin login. A rate limit or refusal on cli-devin stops the run.
3. **Lane authorization.** At most 3 parallel lanes and never two on one file. The operator has already given this.
4. **Conductor placement.** The conductor is a Claude Code session. Execution and verification run on Sonnet 5.5 high subagents, plus cli-pi DeepSeek V4.1 Flash for single-change lanes, and every review runs on cli-devin `swe-2-max`, except the phase 009 closeout review, which the operator moved to cli-codex `gpt-6-luna` at max effort on the fast tier (approved 2026-10-25, recorded in the phase 009 `goal.md` section 4). The benchmark subject is cli-devin `swe-2-max`.
<!-- /ANCHOR:scope -->

## PHASE DOCUMENTATION MAP

> This spec uses phased decomposition. Each phase is an independently executable child spec folder. All implementation details (plan, tasks, checklist, decisions, continuity) live inside the phase children.

| Phase | Folder | Focus | Status |
|-------|--------|-------|--------|
| 1 | 001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/ | Census every template, example and reference defect into a verified ledger and measure the authoring baseline | Complete |
| 2 | 002-template-and-example-defect-repair/ | Apply the ledger rows owned by this phase with a tested exact-match applier | Complete |
| 3 | 003-versioned-placeholder-gate/ | Make unfilled `[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: ...]` tokens a Draft warning and a later error, for v2.3 docs only | Complete |
| 4 | 004-template-section-fill-guidance/ | Add a fill instruction and a good and weak example to each core section, and guard example drift | Complete |
| 5 | 005-weak-model-authoring-card/ | Write the authoring card, route the skill to it and guard the sync | Complete |
| 6 | 006-speckit-command-and-agent-alignment/ | Check and update the speckit and doctor commands and the doc-authoring agents such as markdown for the new templates, gate and card | Complete |
| 7 | 007-playbook-catalog-readme-alignment/ | Update the playbook, catalog and READMEs for the new behavior | Complete |
| 8 | 008-playbook-run-in-worktree/ | Run every new or changed playbook scenario in a disposable worktree and report PASS or FAIL | Complete |
| 9 | 009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/ | Rerun the baseline, report the delta per metric and close the packet | Complete |

### Phase Transition Rules

- Each phase MUST pass `validate.sh` independently before the next phase begins
- Parent spec tracks aggregate progress via this map
- Use `/spec_kit:resume [parent-folder]/[NNN-phase]/` to resume a specific phase
- Run `validate.sh --recursive` on parent to validate all phases as integrated unit

### Phase Handoff Criteria

| From | To | Criteria | Verification |
|------|-----|----------|--------------|
| 001-template-drift-census-and-baseline | 002-template-and-example-defect-repair | `ledger.jsonl` exists, every row reproduces and the baseline holds three runs | `jq -c . ledger.jsonl` exits 0 and `baseline.json` holds 3 runs |
| 002-template-and-example-defect-repair | 003-versioned-placeholder-gate | Every owner-002 row no longer reproduces and the suite result matches the phase 001 start | Row evidence commands print nothing and the suite summary lines match `suite-start.txt` |
| 003-versioned-placeholder-gate | 004-template-section-fill-guidance | Core hints are tokens, the marker is v2.3 and recursive strict counts are unchanged | The failing-first test passes and the before and after counts match |
| 004-template-section-fill-guidance | 005-weak-model-authoring-card | Every core section has guidance, goldens pass and the drift guard passes | The golden test and the drift guard script exit 0 |
| 005-weak-model-authoring-card | 006-speckit-command-and-agent-alignment | The card exists, the router loads it and the sync guard passes | The sync guard script exits 0 and the workflow invariance test passes |
| 006-speckit-command-and-agent-alignment | 007-playbook-catalog-readme-alignment | Every speckit and doctor command and every doc-authoring agent names the card, the v2.3 token and the guard scripts where it describes authoring | The phase 006 audit table has no open row |
| 007-playbook-catalog-readme-alignment | 008-playbook-run-in-worktree | Every new scenario file exists and the playbook index lists it | The playbook index lists each scenario id |
| 008-playbook-run-in-worktree | 009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout | Every scenario has a recorded PASS or an escalated FAIL | The report folder holds a row for each scenario |

<!-- ANCHOR:questions -->
## 4. OPEN QUESTIONS

- Resolved 2026-10-24: LLM Gateway ran out of usage after phase 001, and the operator moved every remaining run to cli-codex `gpt-6-luna` and cli-devin `swe-2-max`. Parent criterion 5 now excludes an optional Level 3 section that a run deleted as its fill note allows. The rule was fixed and committed before any fresh run, as commit `e13115c7da`, and is implemented in `scratch/tools/justified-anchors.sh`. A frozen anchor that is missing from a packet counts, unless it is one of the six optional `tasks.md` anchors in the contract and its condition holds: `arch-verify` when the packet has no `ADR-<n>`, `perf-verify` when `spec.md` has no `NFR-P<n>`, and `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify` and `sign-off` by task definition. `docs-verify` never holds. Any missing anchor in `spec.md`, `plan.md` or `acceptance-criteria.md` counts, and so does any required `tasks.md` anchor.

   128	## 3. METHODOLOGY
   129	
   130	Fixture: one Level 3 packet for a `--quiet` flag on `recommend-level.sh`. The brief is `brief-1.txt` from the SWE 2 Max baseline, copied for each final run and compared with `cmp`, so no word changed. Each side is three serial runs by cli-devin `swe-2-max` on `--permission-mode dangerous` with a 2700 second watchdog. The measured documents are `spec.md`, `plan.md`, `tasks.md` and `acceptance-criteria.md`. The throwaway track is copied outside the worktree and then deleted after each measurement.
   131	
   132	Metrics: `strict_passed` is the count of `RESULT: PASSED` lines from strict validation of the packet. `leftover_hints` counts bare v2.2 hint strings from the baseline list (121 patterns) plus v2.3 value tokens, so each unfilled instruction counts once whichever spelling the template used. `missing_anchors` sums the shortfall of each document against the baseline anchor counts (spec 7, plan 14, tasks 21, acceptance-criteria 3). `out_of_scope_writes` counts new lines in the git status after the run, outside the bench track and this phase folder. `seconds` is the dispatch end second minus the dispatch start second, the same clock the baseline used.
   133	
   134	Second set (third amendment): same brief, same fixture, same five metrics, same scripts, with the model changed to `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` on `--permission-mode dangerous` and a 2700 second watchdog. The final runs ran in this worktree one after another, each with the per-process git setting `core.fsmonitor=false` because a git file-system monitor daemon had stalled `git ls-files` earlier the same day. The DeepSeek baseline was provisioned and run by a separate agent in a disposable worktree at `ac2be31ec6` and its folder is `scratch/amend3/baseline-deepseek/`. Its metrics use the frozen hint and anchor lists.
   135	
   136	Fresh set: the same brief (`cmp` against the phase 1 brief, no difference), the same dispatch on cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` with a 2700 second watchdog, and the same five metrics, except that `missing_anchors` is the counted value from `scratch/tools/justified-anchors.sh`, with the raw shortfall recorded beside it. The runs ran one after another in a disposable worktree made with `worktree-naming.sh create deepseek-final e13115c7da --no-provision`, provisioned with the per-process git setting `core.fsmonitor=false`. The packet folder was deleted in that worktree only, uncommitted, and the `.skilled` diff against the commit was empty before the first run and had the same hash before and after each run. All evidence files were written to this packet's scratch folder, outside the disposable worktree.
   137	
   138	Second fresh set: identical to the first fresh set in brief, dispatch, measures, rule script and isolation, except for the tree (commit `74a629a7af`, which adds the fix-completeness note fix) and the worktree (a new one, `worktrees/224-deepseek-final2`, provisioned with `core.fsmonitor=false`). The `.skilled` diff against the commit was empty before the runs and had the same hash before and after each run. The evidence is in `scratch/amend3/fresh2/`.
   139	
   140	Each run's Devin session was found by working directory and start window, and its tool calls were searched in both the call and the update column for the baseline evidence folder, this phase's scratch folder and `/private/tmp`.

   157	## 5. PROCESS NOTES
   158	
   159	Final runs 1 to 3 ran in order with nothing else touching the worktree. No attempt was discarded, and every dispatch exited with status 0. The audit found exactly one session per run and no tool call naming a forbidden path. The git diff of `.skilled` hashed the same before and after each run, and the status diff showed no write outside the bench track.
   160	
   161	The three final runs took 437, 429, 1822 seconds. The three baseline runs took 326, 317, 384 seconds.
   162	
   163	The report file is `README.md`, not the `benchmark-report.md` that the benchmark authoring skill names, because every existing folder under this reports root holds `README.md` and the index links to the folder.
   164	
   165	Second set (third amendment). All three final dispatches exited with status 0, the `.skilled` diff hash was the same before and after each run, and the status diff showed no write outside the bench track. Each audit found exactly one Devin session. The final runs took 259, 198, 243 seconds and the baseline runs took 242, 217, 298 seconds. The baseline runs happened while the suite rerun of this amendment was going on the same machine, so seconds are not like-for-like. Seconds are reported and are not part of the verdict rule.
   166	
   167	The audit patterns gave hits, and they were read one by one. In runs 1 and 3 the hits are the benchmark's own files: the session ran `git status` and the listing named the untracked evidence folders of this phase, which is why the phase scratch pattern matched in the update column, and run 1 also wrote its own validation output to `/tmp`, which resolves to `/private/tmp`. Neither run read a baseline or phase file. Run 2 is different. Its session tried to read `scratch/baseline/hints-l3.txt` and `scratch/baseline/anchors-l3.txt` in this phase (neither path exists), read the frozen `hints-v2.3.txt` and `anchors-v2.3.txt` that sit in `scratch/baseline/`, and listed `scratch/bench-evidence/`, which holds the earlier runs' documents. Under the protocol rule that any hit marks a run contaminated, run 2 is contaminated and the stop rule fired. The three runs were already finished when this was read, because they ran in one unattended chain, so nothing was rerun. The run is kept as recorded. The evidence is `scratch/amend3/runs/T005-2.audit`, `.calls` and `.updates`. Runs 1 and 3 also have audit hits by the literal rule and are classified benign above, and that classification is a judgment from reading the hits, not a measurement.
   168	
   169	Fresh set. Every dispatch exited with status 0, exactly one Devin session was found per run, the `.skilled` diff hash was the same before and after each run, and no write fell outside the bench track. Seconds were 208, 223, 153, against 217, 242 and 298 on the baseline, and they were not measured under equal load. The audit searched the call and update columns of each session for ten strings (`baseline-swe`, `baseline-deepseek`, `bench-evidence`, `bench-packets`, `hints-`, `anchors-`, `009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout`, `/private/tmp`, `authoring-baseline`, `model-eval`). No call text of any run names one of them. Every hit sits in a tool's output: in all three runs the output of `git status --porcelain`, which lists the uncommitted deletion of this packet's files by path, and in run 2 also the output of a code search for `recommend-level`, which returned one line of the report README that names the fixture. No file of the packet or the report was read, because the packet files do not exist in that worktree. The sessions did see file names, so this is a limit, not a clean zero (section 7). The hit lines are in `scratch/amend3/fresh/runs/T005-N.hits`.
   170	
   171	Second fresh set. Every hit is tool output naming paths: the output of `git status --porcelain`, which lists the uncommitted deletion of this packet's files (all three runs), the output of a file-list search for `recommend-level` that returned the report README path (run 1), and, in run 3, a `git status` after a repair of its own packet.    175	## 6. FINDINGS
   176	
   177	The load-bearing number is `missing_anchors`. Its final maximum is 6 against 0 on the baseline.
   178	Run 3 wrote a `tasks.md` with 15 anchors where the baseline expects 21. Compared with run 1, it lacks `arch-verify`, `perf-verify`, `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `docs-verify`, `sign-off`. Strict validation still passed, so no validator rule catches those sections.
   179	
   180	Scored against the contract-required anchors, all six runs miss 0 of 15 required `tasks.md` anchors. The six anchors run 3 deleted are optional in the contract.
   181	
   182	Leftover hints fell: the final maximum is 0 and the baseline maximum is 1. Strict validation passed in all 3 final runs. No run wrote outside its track.
   183	
   184	Time rose. Seconds per run ranged from 429 to 1822 on the final side against 317 to 384 on the baseline. Two final runs took about as long as each other (437 and 429), and run 3 took 1822.
   185	
   186	Second set (third amendment). The load-bearing number is again `missing_anchors`: a final maximum of 3 against 0. The label sentence did not remove the omission. Run 1 still deleted three of the six optional Level 3 verification sections (`deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `sign-off`) and kept `arch-verify`, `perf-verify` and `docs-verify`. Runs 2 and 3 kept all six, and run 2 is contaminated. Scored against the contract-required anchors, all six DeepSeek runs, three baseline and three final, miss 0 of 15 (`scratch/amend3/diagnostic.txt`, a diagnostic and not a verdict).
   187	
   188	The fill notes of those three sections each carry a 'delete the section when' clause (the packet ships no service, none applies, no sign-off applies). The new sentence says the section stays at Level 3, and the same note then says to delete it under that condition. Whether run 1 acted on those clauses is UNKNOWN, because its reply holds only the final result line. What would confirm it is a transcript that shows the reason, or a rerun with the clauses reworded.
   189	
   190	Leftover hints were 0 in all three final runs against a baseline of 1, 0 and 0. Strict validation passed in all three. Seconds ranged from 198 to 259 on the final side against 217 to 298 on the baseline, with the load difference noted in section 5.
   191	
   192	Fresh set. Counted `missing_anchors` is 1, 0 and 0 (raw 2, 0 and 3). Run 1 left out `fix-completeness` and `sign-off`. The rule excuses `sign-off` and counts `fix-completeness`, because it is a required anchor in the contract. Strict validation still passed, so no validator rule requires that section. The section's fill note says 'Delete the rows that do not apply', and the run deleted the whole section, so this is not a deletion the note allows. Run 3 left out `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify` and `sign-off`, all excused. Run 2 kept all 21 anchors. Scored against the contract-required anchors, run 1 misses 1 of 15 and runs 2 and 3 miss 0 (`scratch/amend3/fresh/diagnostic.txt`, a diagnostic and not a verdict). Leftover hints were 0 in all three runs against a baseline of 1, 0 and 0. Time was lower than the baseline (153 to 223 seconds against 217 to 298), without equal load.
   193	
   194	Across all three final sets the omission moves: swe-2-max run 3 dropped six sections, DeepSeek second-set run 1 dropped three, and DeepSeek fresh run 1 dropped one required section. No set shows the same sections dropped twice, and the sample is too small to tell variation from an effect of the template wording.
   195	
   196	Second fresh set. Counted and raw `missing_anchors` are 0, 0 and 0. The deletion of the `fix-completeness` section that failed the first fresh set did not recur in three runs. Leftover hints were 0, 1 and 0, with the baseline at 1, 0 and 0. Scored against the contract-required anchors, all three runs miss 0 of 15 (`scratch/amend3/fresh2/diagnostic.txt`, a diagnostic). Across all four final sets the dropped sections moved: six, then three, then one required section, then none. That pattern fits a fix that worked and it fits run-to-run variation, and three runs cannot tell them apart.
   197	
   198	---
   199	
   200	## 7. CAVEATS
   201	
   202	Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim. One run produced the whole regression, so the sample cannot say whether the omission comes from the template change or from ordinary run-to-run variation. The fixture is one small feature and four documents. The model is one model at one setting. A template change and a card change landed together, so this report cannot separate their effects.
   203	
   204	The `spec.md`, `plan.md` and `tasks.md` of the three final packets, and the `tasks.md` of the three baseline packets, are kept as `.txt` copies under `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/bench-evidence/`. The rest of each final packet was copied to the conductor scratchpad outside the worktree and is not part of the repository. The raw run logs and audit files are in the source packet named in `source.md`.
   205	
   206	Second set (third amendment): one run produced the whole regression and another is contaminated, so there are two usable clean runs. Three runs cannot separate the label sentence from run-to-run variation, and the first set's single regression and this set's single regression point at the same six sections. The model changed between the sets, so the two sets are not comparable with each other. The DeepSeek baseline and the final runs did not share a worktree, and the final worktree holds earlier benchmark evidence that a session can find (run 2 did), which the baseline worktree did not. The final copies of `spec.md`, `plan.md` and `tasks.md` are kept as `.txt` under `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/amend3/bench-evidence/`.
   207	
   208	Fresh set: it is one worktree with the packet folder removed, three runs, and one model. The sessions could see the names of the deleted files in `git status` output and, in run 2, one report line from a code search, so the isolation is strong for file contents and weaker for file names. The rule was written after the first two final sets were scored, which the parent goal states openly, and the fresh set is the first that was run after the rule was committed. The final copies of `spec.md`, `plan.md` and `tasks.md` are kept as `.txt` under `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/amend3/fresh/bench-evidence/`.
   209	
   210	Second fresh set: it is the fourth final set, run after a template fix aimed at the failure of the third, so the PASS is a result after repeated attempts and not a first-try result. It is one worktree with the packet folder removed, three runs and one model. The sessions saw the names of the deleted files. A different model, a different feature or a Level 1 packet is not measured. The final copies of `spec.md`, `plan.md` and `tasks.md` are kept as `.txt` under `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring/009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/scratch/amend3/fresh2/bench-evidence/`.
   211	
   212	---
   213	
   214	## 8. RECOMMENDATIONS
   215	
   216	- **Apply now.** Keep the already shipped template work as it stands, and do not read that as proof of a gain. Report the result as it is: C5 FAIL on the frozen metric, fewer leftover hints, and one run that deleted six optional Level 3 verification sections.
   217	- **Validate first.** Make the `## L3+:` heading label unambiguous for Level 3 authors, then rerun this benchmark in a new phase with three or more runs on the same brief and see whether the omission recurs.
   218	- **Future.** Decide whether strict validation should treat the optional Level 3 verification anchors as required when a packet records an ADR, a performance requirement or paired documents. That is a validator change and belongs to its own packet.
   219	
   220	Recommendations after the second set, which supersede the first set's 'Validate first' line (verdict FAIL again):
   221	
   222	- **Apply now.** Keep the label sentence and the shipped template work. They do no harm on the measured data (leftover hints 0, strict validation 3 of 3) and there is no evidence that they remove the omission. Do not report criterion 5 as met.
   223	- **Decide the metric.** The operator decided this question with the anchor rule below. Against the contract-required anchors every run on both models and both trees scores 0 missing, so the frozen metric and the contract disagree about what a complete Level 3 `tasks.md` is.
   224	- **Validate first.** Before another rerun, either reword the 'delete the section when' clauses of the three sections run 1 dropped, or accept that deleting them is allowed. Then run the final set in a worktree that holds no earlier benchmark evidence, as the baseline did, so a session cannot find the frozen lists.
   225	- **Future.** A validator rule that requires the optional Level 3 verification anchors when a packet records an ADR, a performance requirement or paired documents is still a separate packet.
   226	
   227	Recommendations after the fresh set, which supersede the two earlier sets' recommendations and are themselves superseded by the next block (verdict FAIL on the set that decided at that time):
   228	
   229	- **Apply now.** Keep the label sentence and the shipped template work. Leftover hints are 0 in all six DeepSeek final runs and strict validation passed in all of them. Do not report criterion 5 as met.
   230	- **Decide what a required anchor is.** `fix-completeness` is required in the contract, yet strict validation passes without it and its fill note invites deleting rows. Either add a validator rule that fails a packet without the required `tasks.md` anchors, or move `fix-completeness` to the optional set with a stated condition. That is a template and validator decision and belongs to its own packet.
   231	- **Validate first.** Reword the `fix-completeness` fill note so it cannot be read as permission to delete the section, then rerun the final set in a fresh disposable worktree. The rule script and the worktree recipe are in the source packet and can be reused unchanged.
   232	- **Future.** A larger run count would separate variation from an effect. Three runs per set cannot.
   233	
   234	Recommendations after the second fresh set, which supersede the earlier ones (verdict PASS on the deciding set):
   235	
   236	- **Apply now.** Ship the template changes as they are: the value tokens, the section fill guidance, the authoring card, the `L3+` label sentence and the `fix-completeness` note fix. Criterion 5 is met on the deciding set.
   237	- **Validate first.** Nothing blocks shipping. A reader who wants more than a range should rerun the final set with more runs and a second model.
   238	- **Future.** Strict validation still does not enforce required anchors, so a packet without a required `tasks.md` section can pass. That is an open follow-up and not fixed here. It belongs to its own packet, together with the `ANCHORS_VALID` rule.
   239

   243

   245	bash .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 3 --track zz-bench-hints --short-name recommend-level-quiet-flag "Add a quiet flag to recommend-level.sh"
   246	rg -o --no-filename "\[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: [^]]*\]" specs/zz-bench-hints/001-recommend-level-quiet-flag/*.md | sort -u > hints.txt
   247	```
   248	
   249	The dispatch and the five metric commands are in `scratch/tools/run-final.sh` in the source packet. The second set used `scratch/tools/run-amend3.sh` in the same way with the DeepSeek model, `scratch/tools/amend3-delta.sh` for the delta and `scratch/tools/amend3-diagnostic.sh` for the diagnostic. The second fresh set used `scratch/tools/run-fresh2.sh`, `scratch/tools/fresh2-delta.sh` and `scratch/tools/fresh2-diagnostic.sh`. The first fresh set used `scratch/tools/run-fresh.sh`, `scratch/tools/fresh-delta.sh`, `scratch/tools/fresh-diagnostic.sh` and the rule script `scratch/tools/justified-anchors.sh`. Run it as `run-final.sh N dispatch` and then `run-final.sh N measure`. The three final runs took about 45 minutes together.

    13	# Feature Specification: Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout
    14	
    15	<!-- SPECKIT_LEVEL: 3 -->
    16	
    17	
    18	---
    19	
    20	## EXECUTIVE SUMMARY
    21	
    22	Phase 1 first measured a DeepSeek V4.1 Flash worker on a Level 2 packet, and the parent later replaced that with a Level 3 baseline on cli-devin swe-2-max, taken before any template changed. This phase measures the same Level 3 task the same way on the final tree and reports the difference for five metrics, each as a minimum and a maximum over three runs. It then publishes the benchmark report, writes the changelogs, regenerates the trigger index, refreshes the track roots and closes the packet against the seven criteria in `../goal.md` section 3.
    23	
    24	**Key Decisions**: the frozen task text is copied byte for byte from phase 1, and leftover hints are counted as bare v2.2 hints plus v2.3 value tokens so the two sides share one definition.
    25	
    26	**Critical Dependencies**: phases 1 to 8 are closed, the write-recipe child is closed and the launch preconditions in `../spec.md` section 3 are met and recorded in `../goal.md` section 4.
    27

    49	## Phase Context
    50	
    51	This is **Phase 9** of the Make system-spec-kit templates and authoring docs followable by a DeepSeek V4.1 Flash agent swarm specification.
    52	
    53	**Scope Boundary**: Edits land in this phase folder, in the benchmark report folder, in the two changelog locations, in the trigger index artifacts, in the `system-speckit` track metadata, in the parent `spec.md` and `goal.md`, and in one row of the `041-write-recipe-fixes` phase map. No template, validator, rule, command, agent or playbook file changes. This is the only phase allowed to edit the parent `spec.md`, the parent `goal.md` and the 041 phase map. The exact rows are the nine status rows of the parent phase map for phases 1 to 9, the seven completion checkboxes in parent `goal.md` section 3, the progress and log rows in parent `goal.md` section 4, and the row for `006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination` in the 041 phase map.
    54	
    55	**Dependencies**:
    56	- The launch preconditions in `../spec.md` section 3 are met and recorded in `../goal.md` section 4.
    57	- Phases 1 to 8 are closed. Phase 1 leaves `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline/baseline.json` and `hints-v2.2.txt`. Phase 3 leaves the frozen folder list in `../003-versioned-placeholder-gate/scratch/census/`. Phase 4 leaves `check-example-drift.sh`. Phase 5 leaves `check-card-sync.sh`. Phase 8 leaves one playbook run report.
    58	- The write-recipe child `specs/system-speckit/041-write-recipe-fixes/006-fix-write-recipe-move-destination/` is closed and passes strict validation.
    59	
    60	**Deliverables**:
    61	- `scratch/baseline/hints-v2.3.txt` and `scratch/baseline/anchors-v2.3.txt`: the final token vocabulary and anchor counts from a fresh Level 3 scaffold.
    62	- `scratch/baseline/runs.jsonl` and `scratch/baseline/baseline.json`: three final runs, each with five metrics.
    63	- `scratch/baseline/delta.json` and `scratch/baseline/delta.md`: the minimum, the maximum and the mean of each metric per side, with the delta.
    64	- `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/benchmark/reports/$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline/`: `README.md`, `results.csv` and `source.md`, plus one new index row in the reports `README.md`.
    65	- `.skilled/changelog/system-spec-kit/v2.9.0.0.md`: the global changelog entry.
    66	- `../changelog/changelog-054-root.md` and `../changelog/changelog-054-009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout.md`: the packet-local changelogs.
    67	- `scratch/closeout/criteria.txt`: one PASS line with its command output for each of the seven parent criteria.
    68	- `scratch/review/T016.txt`: the frontier review reply, one line per finding or `NO FINDINGS`.
    69	
    70	**Changelog**:
    71	- This phase writes the matching file in `../changelog/` with the nested generator and the global component entry in `.skilled/changelog/system-spec-kit/`.
    72	<!-- /ANCHOR:phase-context -->
    73	
    74	---
    75	
    76	<!-- ANCHOR:problem -->
    77	## 2. PROBLEM & PURPOSE
    78	
    79	### Problem Statement
    80	Phases 1 to 8 change the templates, the validator, the examples, the authoring card, the commands and the playbooks. Without a second measurement on the same task, the packet can only claim that the files changed. It cannot say whether a weak model leaves fewer unfilled hints, hits the required anchors more often or writes outside its track less often. The packet also cannot close while the generated index, the track roots, the changelogs and the parent maps still describe a planned state.
    81	
    82	### Purpose
    83	The closing report states the before and after per metric as a range, and the packet closes with every generated artifact and every status row reconciled to one observed state.
    84	<!-- /ANCHOR:problem -->
    85	
    86	---
    87	
    88	<!-- ANCHOR:scope -->
    89	## 3. SCOPE
    90	
    91	### In Scope
    92	- Three serial final runs of the frozen authoring task from `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/tasks.md` T009, with no word changed.
    93	- The five metrics M1 to M5, measured by the same commands on both sides, with M2 extended to count v2.3 value tokens.
    94	- A delta report with the minimum, the maximum and the mean of each metric per side, and an explicit statement that three runs cannot support a significance claim.
    95	- One benchmark report folder that follows the run folder grammar and the ten-section report contract.
    96	- A global changelog entry and two packet-local changelog files.
    97	- One trigger index regeneration and one track root refresh.
    98	- Reconciliation of the parent phase map, the parent goal log, the 041 phase map row and the completion metadata of this phase.
    99	- One proof line per parent completion criterion, written before any parent goal box is ticked.
   100	- One read-only frontier review of the closeout diff.
   101	
   102	### Out of Scope
   103	- Any template, validator, rule, command, agent or playbook edit. Phases 2 to 8 own those.
   104	- Any change to the frozen task text, the four measured documents or the metric definitions. A change makes the delta meaningless.
   105	- A significance claim. Three runs per side cannot support one.
   106	- A push. This phase ends in one local commit on the worktree branch and nothing is pushed.
   107	- Fixing another phase folder. A child that fails strict validation is named and the closeout stops, because another phase owns its docs.
   108

   173	## 5. SUCCESS CRITERIA
   174	
   175	- **SC-001**: The report answers "did the work help" with a minimum and a maximum per metric on both sides, and a reader can recompute every number from one command.
   176	- **SC-002**: The operator can review the local commit and push the whole packet without editing a generated artifact, a status row or a map first.
   177	- **SC-003**: A later packet can rerun the same report by copying `tasks.md` T004 to T008 with a new date.

   182	<!-- ANCHOR:risks -->
   183	## 6. RISKS & DEPENDENCIES
   184	
   185	| Type | Item | Impact | Mitigation |
   186	|------|------|--------|------------|
   187	| Dependency | Phases 1 to 8 closed | There is no final tree to measure and no report from phase 8 | `tasks.md` T002 checks nine closeable statements and stops the phase when one is missing |
   188	| Dependency | LLM Gateway provider for `llmgateway/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017` | No final run can start | T001 checks `opencode.json` before the first dispatch and records the error when it fails |
   189	| Dependency | Phase 8 playbook report | Parent criterion 6 cannot be proven | T015 names the missing report and leaves the criterion unticked |
   190	| Risk | The three final runs vary so much that the delta is noise | The report overclaims | The report prints the range and states that three runs cannot support a significance claim |
   191	| Risk | A final run writes outside its throwaway track | The tree is dirty and the next phase inherits it | M4 counts those writes and the conductor restores each path before the next run |
   192	| Risk | The report folder name uses the authoring date | The index row misdates the run | T008 takes the date from `date +%F` at execution time and validates the name with the kebab checker |
   193	| Risk | A phase 1 to 8 document fails recursive strict | The packet cannot close | T017 names the failing child and stops. This phase does not edit another phase folder |
   194	<!-- /ANCHOR:risks -->
   195	
   196	---
   197	
   198	
   199	## 7. NON-FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS
   200	
   201	### Performance
   202	- **NFR-P01**: Each final run is one Task call. Three runs run serially. A run that fails is recorded and dispatched once more.
   203	
   204	### Security
   205	- **NFR-S01**: Every brief carries no credential, token or key. Workers inherit the session model and its provider login.
   206	
   207	### Reliability
   208	- **NFR-R01**: Every number in the report is produced by a command in `tasks.md`, so a second conductor on the same tree gets the same numbers from the same runs.
   209	
   210	---
   211	
   212	## 8. EDGE CASES
   213	
   214	### Data Boundaries
   215	- Empty input: a final run that leaves a document empty still counts as a run. M2 and M3 record the result. The report does not drop the run.
   216	- Maximum length: a metric value that is not an integer is written in `delta.md` to one decimal place and never rounded in `delta.json`.
   217	
   218	### Error Scenarios
   219	- External service failure: a provider refusal stops the dispatch. The conductor records the error, retries once after 10 minutes and then stops the phase with the failed run named.
   220	- Network timeout: handled as in NFR-P01.
   221	- Missing phase 8 report: T015 prints `C6 MISSING-008-REPORT`, the parent criterion stays unticked and the closeout stops.
   222

    67	| AC-014 | REQ-014 | Given the closed summary, When T010 has run, Then the operator handoff block names the push as the operator's step and states that the phase made one local commit and pushed nothing | `grep -c 'Operator handoff' implementation-summary.md` prints `1` and `grep -c 'one local commit' implementation-summary.md` prints `1` | Met | - |
    68	| AC-015 | REQ-015 | Given the closed packet, When T010 and T017 have run, Then spec, acceptance rows, continuity, goal and summary claim one state | `rg -c '\| Met \| - \|' acceptance-criteria.md` prints `15`, every completed document reads `Complete`, and `completion_pct` reads `100` | Met | - |
    69	
    70	### Status values
    71	
    72	| Value | Meaning |
    73	|-------|---------|
    74	| `Met` | Verified. The Verification cell names evidence that was actually observed. |
    75	| `Unmet` | Not yet satisfied. Blocks closure. |
    76	| `Waived` | Deliberately not pursued. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |
    77	| `Superseded` | Replaced by a different criterion or decision. Requires an ADR in the Waiver cell. |
    78	
    79	### Waiver cell
    80	
    81	Write `-` when the row is `Met` or `Unmet`. Write `ADR-NNN` when the row is
    82	`Waived` or `Superseded`, naming a decision record that exists in
    83	`decision-record.md`. A waiver naming an ADR that is not there fails validation:
    84	the point of a waiver is that someone recorded the reasoning, so an unbacked
    85	waiver is treated as an unmet criterion rather than as a pass.

    48	## What Was Built
    49	
    50	The packet now ends with a measured before and after, and the measurement found one failure. Three final runs of the frozen Level 3 authoring task are recorded beside the SWE 2 Max baseline. Every final run passed strict validation, the final maximum of `leftover_hints` is 0 against a baseline maximum of 1, and no run wrote outside its track. Parent criterion 5 is FAIL on the frozen metric, because run 3 left 15 anchors in `tasks.md` where the baseline expects 21, so `missing_anchors` has a final maximum of 6 against 0.
    51	
    52	The six anchors run 3 deleted are the optional Level 3 verification sections: `arch-verify`, `perf-verify`, `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `docs-verify` and `sign-off`. A diagnostic, chosen after the result and not a verdict, scores all six runs against the anchors the contract requires (`template-structure.js contract 3 tasks.md` lists 15 required and these 6 optional), and every run misses none. Three of the deletions went against the template's own fill notes, because the packet records ADR-001 (`plan.md` line 239 of run 3, kept as `scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/plan.txt`), states NFR-P01 (`spec.md` line 133 of run 3, kept as `scratch/bench-evidence/final-run-3/spec.txt`) and holds paired documents. The run's reply says "L3+ optional sections dropped (this is Level 3, not 3+)", so it read the `## L3+:` heading prefix as Level 3+ only. Run 3 also took 1822 seconds against 437 and 429, and the cause is unknown. The run log is `scratch/baseline/runs.jsonl`, the extras are in `runs-meta.jsonl` and the delta is `scratch/baseline/delta.json`.
    53	
    54	### Third amendment: label fix and second final set
    55	
    56	The fill note under each of the six `## L3+:` headings of the `tasks.md` template now opens with 'L3+ means Level 3 and above, so this section stays at Level 3.' The phase 004 plan rows, the golden snapshot and the guards match it, and the full suite passed again with the same counts. The operator ruled out the swe-2 models, so a new DeepSeek baseline of three runs at the packet start commit was taken by a separate agent, and three final runs ran on cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` on the amended tree. Strict validation passed in all three and leftover hints were 0 in all three, but run 1 wrote a `tasks.md` without `deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify` and `sign-off`, so `missing_anchors` has a final maximum of 3 against a baseline maximum of 0 and criterion 5 fails again. Run 2 is contaminated, because its session read the frozen vocabulary files, and it is kept in the numbers and labelled. The first final set stays on record as history, measured on the earlier tree on swe-2-max.
    57	
    58	### New anchor rule and fresh runs
    59	
    60	The operator then chose "New rule, fresh runs". A rule script, `scratch/tools/justified-anchors.sh`, was committed with its scores on the twelve earlier packets before any fresh run: a frozen anchor missing from a packet counts unless it is an optional Level 3 `tasks.md` anchor whose fill-note condition holds. The first swe-2-max final set scores 0, 0 and 3 under it and the second DeepSeek set scores 0, 0 and 0. Three fresh runs then ran on cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` in a disposable worktree with this packet's folder removed from its working tree. All three passed strict validation with no leftover hints, but run 1 deleted the whole required `fix-completeness` section, so counted `missing_anchors` is 1, 0 and 0 against a baseline maximum of 0 and criterion 5 fails for the third time. Run 3's deleted sections (`deploy-ready`, `compliance-verify`, `sign-off`) are excused by the rule.
    61	
    62	### Fix-completeness note fixed and a second fresh set
    63	
    64	The cause of the third failure was found and fixed at the source: strict validation does not check that required anchors are present, and the required `fix-completeness` fill note said 'Delete the rows that do not apply', which the failing run read as permission to delete the section. The note now says to keep the section and delete only rows. The anchor rule and criterion 5 did not change. After the full suite passed again with the same counts, a second fresh set ran on cli-devin `deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max` in a new disposable worktree with this packet's folder removed. All three runs passed strict validation, leftover hints were 0, 1 and 0 against a baseline maximum of 1, counted `missing_anchors` was 0, 0 and 0 against 0, and nothing was written outside the track. **Criterion 5 passes on this fourth final set.** The three earlier sets failed and stay on record, each labelled with its model, tree and rule. The PASS followed a fix made after a failure was understood, so it is not a first-try result, and three runs on one model give a range and not an effect size.
    65	
    66	### Phase 9: authoring-benchmark-and-closeout

   138	<!-- ANCHOR:limitations -->
   139	## Known Limitations
   140	
   141	1. **Criterion 5 failed three times before it passed.** `missing_anchors` had a final maximum of 6, 3 and (counted under the new rule) 1 against 0 on the first three final sets, and the fourth set passed. The result is a range over three runs on one model after a targeted template fix.
   142	2. **The fix was made after the failure.** The `fix-completeness` note was corrected after a run deleted that section, and the next set passed. The report states this plainly, and a larger run count and a second model would be needed to separate a fix from variation.
   143	3. **Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim.** One run produced the whole failure, so the sample cannot separate the template change from run-to-run variation.
   144	4. **The fixture is one small feature and four documents.** A different feature shape or a Level 1 packet is not measured.
   145	5. **Acceptance rows were Unmet until criterion 5 passed.** AC-010, AC-011 and AC-015 needed nine Complete map rows, seven PASS lines and a Complete status, and they are Met now.
   146	6. **The validator does not enforce required anchors.** Strict validation checks the form of anchors and not that each required `tasks.md` anchor is present, so a packet without the required `fix-completeness` section passed. The template note was corrected to say to keep the section, and the validator rule is an open follow-up that is not fixed here.

   116	| Run protocol on 2026-10-25 | The runs mirror the script that produced the SWE 2 Max baseline. Each dispatch uses the baseline brief-1.txt copied byte for byte (cmp), runs under a 2700 second watchdog on cli-devin swe-2-max with --permission-mode dangerous, and records its exit status. M5 is the dispatch end second minus the dispatch start second, as in the baseline, not the measurement time. M2 is the bare hint count (hints-l3.txt) plus the value token count. M4 is the status diff, and a change in the hash of git diff -- .skilled before and after a run also counts as an out-of-scope write. Each run's packet is copied to the scratchpad outside the worktree before it is removed. The session audit selects Devin sessions in the worktree between the start second and the end second plus 60, requires exactly one, and searches both tool_call_json and tool_call_update_json. A rate-limit or billing reply is not a run and is redone for the same run number. Extra per-run fields (exit status, bare and token split, audit) are in scratch/baseline/runs-meta.jsonl so baseline.json keeps the six keys per run. |

   152	  cd "$WORKTREE"
   153	  B="$BL/baseline.json"
   154	  F="$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
   155	  jq -n --slurpfile b "$B" --slurpfile f "$F" '
   156	    def stat($runs; $k):
   157	      {min: ($runs | map(.[$k]) | min),
   158	       max: ($runs | map(.[$k]) | max),
   159	       mean: (($runs | map(.[$k]) | add) / ($runs | length))};
   160	    ["strict_passed","leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes","seconds"] as $keys
   161	    | {metrics: ($keys | map(. as $k | {metric: $k, baseline: stat($b[0].runs; $k), final: stat($f[0].runs; $k)}))}
   162	    | .metrics |= map(. + {delta_mean: (.final.mean - .baseline.mean)})
   163	  ' > "$SC/baseline/delta.json"
   164	  jq -r '
   165	    def one: (.*10 | round) / 10;
   166	    "# Authoring delta\n",
   167	    "- Baseline: three Level 3 runs of the frozen brief on cli-devin swe-2-max, recorded in `../001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/scratch/baseline-swe-l3/baseline.json`.",
   168	    "- Final: three runs of the same task, recorded in `scratch/baseline/baseline.json`.",
   169	    "- Each cell is the minimum, the maximum and the mean over three runs.",
   170	    "- Three runs cannot support a significance claim. The table reports a range, not an effect size.\n",
   171	    "| Metric | Baseline min | Baseline max | Baseline mean | Final min | Final max | Final mean | Delta of means |",
   172	    "|--------|--------------|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|",
   173	    (.metrics[] | "| \(.metric) | \(.baseline.min) | \(.baseline.max) | \(.baseline.mean | one) | \(.final.min) | \(.final.max) | \(.final.mean | one) | \(.delta_mean | one) |")
   174	  ' "$SC/baseline/delta.json" > "$SC/baseline/delta.md"
   175	  jq -e 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max))' "$SC/baseline/delta.json"
   176	  ```

   178	- [x] T008 Write the benchmark report and its index row (`$S/benchmark/reports/$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline/`, `$S/benchmark/reports/README.md`). Read `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/spec.md`, `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/plan.md` and `../008-playbook-run-in-worktree/implementation-summary.md` for the report shape phase 8 accepted, and read `.skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-benchmark/SKILL.md` sections 3 and 5 for the required package shape and the report contract, before composing the report. Run the block below, which writes `results.csv`, `README.md` and `source.md` from `delta.json` and inserts one index row. Verify: `awk 'NR>1' "$REPORT/results.csv" | wc -l` prints `6`, `grep -c '^## [0-9]' "$REPORT/README.md"` prints `10`, `grep -c 'model-eval--authoring-baseline' "$S/benchmark/reports/README.md"` prints `1`, and the script prints `NAME_OK`.
   179	
   180	  ```bash
   181	  cd "$WORKTREE"
   182	  DATE=$(date +%F)
   183	  NAME="$DATE--model-eval--authoring-baseline"
   184	  REPORT="$S/benchmark/reports/$NAME"
   185	  DELTA="$SC/baseline/delta.json"
   186	  B="$BL/baseline.json"
   187	  F="$SC/baseline/baseline.json"
   188	  echo "$NAME" | grep -qE '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}--[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*--[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$' && echo NAME_OK
   189	  mkdir -p "$REPORT"
   190	  {
   191	    echo 'side,run,strict_passed,leftover_hints,missing_anchors,out_of_scope_writes,seconds'
   192	    jq -r '.runs[] | "baseline,\(.run),\(.strict_passed),\(.leftover_hints),\(.missing_anchors),\(.out_of_scope_writes),\(.seconds)"' "$B"
   193	    jq -r '.runs[] | "final,\(.run),\(.strict_passed),\(.leftover_hints),\(.missing_anchors),\(.out_of_scope_writes),\(.seconds)"' "$F"
   194	  } > "$REPORT/results.csv"
   195	  val() { jq -r --arg m "$1" --arg s "$2" --arg k "$3" '.metrics[] | select(.metric==$m) | .[$s][$k]' "$DELTA"; }
   196	  L_B_MIN=$(val leftover_hints baseline min); L_B_MAX=$(val leftover_hints baseline max)
   197	  L_F_MIN=$(val leftover_hints final min);   L_F_MAX=$(val leftover_hints final max)
   198	  VERDICT=$(jq -r 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max)) | if . then "PASS" else "FAIL" end' "$DELTA")
   199	  {
   200	    printf '# %s\n\n' "$NAME"
   201	    printf '> system-spec-kit · model-eval · authoring-baseline · cli-devin swe-2-max\n\n---\n\n'
   202	    printf '## 1. HEADLINE / OVERVIEW\n\n'
   203	    printf '**Verdict: %s**\n\n' "$VERDICT"
   204	    printf 'Three baseline runs and three final runs of one frozen Level 3 authoring brief. Leftover hints run from %s to %s on the baseline side and from %s to %s on the final side. The verdict is PASS when every final run passes strict validation and none is worse than the worst baseline run on leftover hints, missing anchors or out-of-scope writes.\n\n' "$L_B_MIN" "$L_B_MAX" "$L_F_MIN" "$L_F_MAX"
   205	    printf '## 2. AGGREGATE RESULTS\n\n'
   206	    echo '| Metric | Baseline min | Baseline max | Baseline mean | Final min | Final max | Final mean | Delta of means |'
   207	    echo '|--------|--------------|--------------|---------------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|'
   208	    jq -r 'def one: (.*10 | round) / 10; .metrics[] | "| \(.metric) | \(.baseline.min) | \(.baseline.max) | \(.baseline.mean | one) | \(.final.min) | \(.final.max) | \(.final.mean | one) | \(.delta_mean | one) |"' "$DELTA"
   209	    printf '\n## 3. METHODOLOGY\n\n'
   210	    cat <<'MD' | sed 's/^  //'
   211	  Fixture: one Level 3 packet for a `--quiet` flag on `recommend-level.sh`. The brief is frozen and copied from the phase 1 baseline with no word changed. Each side is three serial runs by cli-devin `swe-2-max`, and each run's Devin session is audited for reads of baseline evidence. The measured documents are `spec.md`, `plan.md`, `tasks.md` and `acceptance-criteria.md`. The throwaway track is deleted after each measurement.
   212	
   213	  ## 4. PER-CANDIDATE PROFILES
   214	
   215	  | Side | Tree | What the worker saw |
   216	  |---|---|---|
   217	  | baseline | Before the packet changes | v2.2 templates, bare bracketed hints, the hand-written examples |
   218	  | final | After phases 1 to 8 | v2.3 value tokens, section fill guidance, the authoring card, the guards |
   219	
   220	  ## 5. PROCESS NOTES
   221	
   222	  The baseline side ran in phase 1 on templates identical to the start commit and was not rerun. The final side ran after every phase closed, so the worker saw the tree the packet ships. A run that left its track behind was restored before the next dispatch.
   223	
   224	  ## 6. FINDINGS
   225	
   226	  The load-bearing numbers are the worst final run on leftover hints, missing anchors and out-of-scope writes, read against the worst baseline run. The baseline already reached 0 hints in one run, so the packet claims no regression rather than a lower minimum. A wide final range means the templates help some runs and not others.
   227	
   228	  ## 7. CAVEATS
   229	
   230	  Three runs per side cannot support a significance claim. The fixture is one small feature and four documents. The model is one model at one effort. A template change and a card change landed together, so this report cannot separate their effects.
   231	
   232	  ## 8. RECOMMENDATIONS
   233	
   234	  - **Apply now.** Keep the v2.3 token gate and the card. Both are measured on the same task.
   235	  - **Validate first.** Rerun this report after the next template change with a new date and the same variant.
   236	  - **Future.** Add a second feature shape if a future packet needs to know whether the gain holds outside one small feature.

   291	  # v2.9.0.0, Templates a Weak Model Can Follow
   292	
   293	  A weak model can now see what a template still needs, because every hint in the five core templates is a value token that fails validation once the packet leaves Draft. The four authoring intents also load a short card rather than the long guide, and the examples no longer teach the defects the old text carried.
   294	
   295	  > Spec folder: `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring` (Level 2)
   296	
   297	  &nbsp;
   298	
   299	  ## What's New at a Glance
   300	
   301	  - **An unfilled hint can no longer pass as finished.** A doc stamped v2.3 warns while the spec is Draft and fails strict validation at any other status.
   302	  - **Every core section says how to fill it.** Each section carries a short instruction and a good and weak example.
   303	  - **Authoring starts on one page.** The router loads a short card for the four authoring intents.
   304	  - **The examples stop teaching the old defects.** Their descriptions, stale checklist lines and anchors now match the templates.
   305	  - **Commands and agents name the new gate and the card.** A speckit or doctor run describes the same authoring path a worker follows.
   306	  - **The playbook and catalog cover the new behavior.** A focused run report records each scenario result.
   307	  - **The result is measured, not asserted.** Three runs of one frozen authoring task on each side give a range for five metrics.

   540	  ```bash
   541	  cd "$WORKTREE"
   542	  C="$SC/closeout/criteria.txt"
   543	  : > "$C"
   544	
   545	  # C1: recursive strict on the packet.
   546	  OUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PKT" --recursive --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1)
   547	  if [ "$OUT" = "RESULT: PASSED" ]; then echo "C1 PASS $OUT" >> "$C"; else echo "C1 FAIL $OUT" >> "$C"; fi
   548	
   549	  # C2: the token gate on a fresh v2.3 packet and on an old stamp.
   550	  rm -rf specs/zz-bench-closeout
   551	  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/create.sh --skip-branch --level 1 --track zz-bench-closeout --short-name gate "Gate" > "$SC/closeout/c2-create.txt" 2>&1

   554	  perl -pi -e 's/\| \*\*Status\*\* \| Draft \|/| **Status** | Complete |/' $P/spec.md
   555	  K=$(env SPECKIT_RULES=PLACEHOLDER_FILLED bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep -c 'placeholder(s) found')
   556	  perl -pi -e 's/\| \*\*Status\*\* \| Complete \|/| **Status** | Draft |/' $P/spec.md
   557	  perl -pi -e 's/\| v2\.3 -->/| v2.2 -->/' $P/spec.md $P/plan.md $P/tasks.md
   558	  V=$(env SPECKIT_RULES=PLACEHOLDER_FILLED bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh $P --strict 2>&1 | grep -c 'placeholder(s) found')
   559	  rm -rf specs/zz-bench-closeout
   560	  if [ "$D" = "RESULT: PASSED" ] && [ "$K" -ge 1 ] && [ "$V" -ge 1 ]; then echo "C2 PASS draft=$D complete_errors=$K v22_errors=$V" >> "$C"; else echo "C2 FAIL draft=$D complete_errors=$K v22_errors=$V" >> "$C"; fi
   561	
   562	  # C3: the frozen phase 3 census, rerun on the final tree.
   563	  ORCH="$S/runtime/dist/lib/validation/orchestrator.js"
   564	  for i in 0 1 2; do env -u SPECKIT_RULES node "$P3/scratch/tools/strict-census.mjs" "$ORCH" "$P3/scratch/census/shard-$i.txt" > "$SC/closeout/b-final-$i.tsv" 2> "$SC/closeout/b-final-$i.err" & done
   565	  wait
   566	  cat "$SC/closeout/b-final-"[012].tsv | LC_ALL=C sort > "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv"
   567	  cut -f1,2,5 "$P3/scratch/census/b-before.tsv" > "$SC/closeout/b-before.cut"
   568	  cut -f1,2,5 "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv" > "$SC/closeout/b-final.cut"
   569	  DIFFROWS=$(diff "$SC/closeout/b-before.cut" "$SC/closeout/b-final.cut" | grep -c '^[<>]')
   570	  BCOUNT=$(cut -f2 "$P3/scratch/census/b-before.tsv" | sort | uniq -c | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ' ')
   571	  FCOUNT=$(cut -f2 "$SC/closeout/b-final.tsv" | sort | uniq -c | tr -s ' ' | tr '\n' ' ')
   572	  if [ "$DIFFROWS" = "0" ]; then echo "C3 PASS CENSUS_SAME gate B before[$BCOUNT] after[$FCOUNT]" >> "$C"; else echo "C3 FAIL diff_rows=$DIFFROWS before[$BCOUNT] after[$FCOUNT]" >> "$C"; fi
   573	
   574	  # C4: the two guards.
   575	  AOUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/check-example-drift.sh 2>&1); AR=$?
   576	  A=$(printf '%s\n' "$AOUT" | tail -1)
   577	  BOUT=$(bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/check-card-sync.sh 2>&1); BR=$?
   578	  B=$(printf '%s\n' "$BOUT" | tail -1)
   579	  if [ "$A" = "EXAMPLE_DRIFT_OK" ] && [ "$B" = "CARD SYNC OK" ] && [ "$AR" = "0" ] && [ "$BR" = "0" ]; then echo "C4 PASS $A $B" >> "$C"; else echo "C4 FAIL $A rc=$AR $B rc=$BR" >> "$C"; fi
   580	
   581	  # C5: the report covers five metrics on two sides of three runs, and no final run regressed against the worst baseline run.
   582	  N=$(jq '.metrics | length' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
   583	  BRUNS=$(jq '.runs | length' "$BL/baseline.json")
   584	  FRUNS=$(jq '.runs | length' "$SC/baseline/baseline.json")
   585	  LOW=$(jq -r '([.metrics[] | select(.metric=="leftover_hints")][0]) | "\(.baseline.max) -> \(.final.max)"' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
   586	  OK=$(jq -r 'def m($k): [.metrics[] | select(.metric==$k)][0]; . as $d | ($d | m("strict_passed").final.min == 1) and (["leftover_hints","missing_anchors","out_of_scope_writes"] | all(. as $k | $d | m($k) | .final.max <= .baseline.max)) | if . then "yes" else "no" end' "$SC/baseline/delta.json")
   587	  if [ "$N" = "5" ] && [ "$BRUNS" = "3" ] && [ "$FRUNS" = "3" ] && [ "$OK" = "yes" ]; then echo "C5 PASS metrics=$N runs=$BRUNS+$FRUNS leftover_max=$LOW" >> "$C"; else echo "C5 FAIL metrics=$N runs=$BRUNS+$FRUNS leftover_max=$LOW" >> "$C"; fi
   588	
   589	  # C6: every playbook scenario this packet added has a PASS or an escalated FAIL.
   590	  REPORTS="$S/benchmark/reports"
   591	  REPORT=$(for d in "$REPORTS"/*--manual-testing-playbook--*/; do test -s "$d/source.md" && rg -q '054-weak-model-spec-authoring' "$d/source.md" && echo "$d"; done | sort | tail -1)
   592	  ADDED=$({ git diff --name-only "$START" -- "$S/manual-testing-playbook/"; git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- "$S/manual-testing-playbook/"; } | sort -u | rg '\.md$' | rg -v 'manual-testing-playbook\.md$')
   593	  MISS=0; LINES=""
   594	  if [ -z "$REPORT" ]; then echo "C6 FAIL MISSING-008-REPORT" >> "$C"; elif [ -z "$ADDED" ]; then echo "C6 FAIL NO_SCENARIO_FILES" >> "$C"; else
   595	    for f in $ADDED; do
   596	      SLUG=$(basename "$(dirname "$f")")-$(basename "$f" .md)
   597	      NUM=$(rg -m1 -o '^title: "([0-9]+)' -r '$1' "$f")
   598	      V=$(awk -F, -v id="$SLUG" -v num="$NUM" 'NR==1{for(i=1;i<=NF;i++){if($i=="scenario_id")s=i; if($i=="verdict")v=i}; next} ($s==id || $s==num){print $v}' "$REPORT/results.csv" | head -1)
   599	      if [ "$V" != "PASS" ]; then
   600	        if rg -q 'FAIL' "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md" && { rg -q --fixed-strings "$SLUG" "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md" || rg -q --fixed-strings "$NUM" "$PKT/008-playbook-run-in-worktree/goal.md"; }; then V="FAIL-LOGGED"; else MISS=$((MISS+1)); fi
   601	      fi
   602	      LINES="$LINES $SLUG=$V"
   603	    done
   604	    if [ "$MISS" = "0" ]; then echo "C6 PASS$LINES" >> "$C"; else echo "C6 FAIL missing=$MISS$LINES" >> "$C"; fi
   605	  fi
   606	
   607	  # C7: swarm commits stay local and nothing is pushed.
   608	  HEAD=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
   609	  COUNT=$(git log --oneline "$START..HEAD" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
   610	  REMOTE=$(git branch -r --contains HEAD | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
   611	  if [ "$COUNT" = "0" ] || [ "$REMOTE" = "0" ]; then echo "C7 PASS head=$HEAD commits_since_start=$COUNT remote_branches_with_head=$REMOTE" >> "$C"; else echo "C7 FAIL head=$HEAD start=$START commits_since_start=$COUNT remote_branches_with_head=$REMOTE" >> "$C"; fi
   612	
   613	  cat "$C"
   614	  PASSES=$(grep -c '^C[1-7] PASS ' "$C")
   615	  N="$PASSES" perl -0pi -e 's{^(\| BASE_REF \|.*\n)}{$1 . "| Parent criteria | Done | $ENV{N} PASS lines in scratch/closeout/criteria.txt |\n"}me unless /\| Parent criteria \|/' "$PKT/goal.md"
   616	  echo "CRITERIA_LOGGED passes=$PASSES"
   617	  ```

   665	    perl -pi -e 's/^    completion_pct: \d+$/    completion_pct: 100/' "$PHASE/goal.md"
   666	    echo PARENT_TICKED_7
   667	  else
   668	    echo "PARENT_NOT_TICKED passes=$(grep -c '^C[1-7] PASS ' "$SC/closeout/criteria.txt")"
   669	  fi
   670	  node .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/repair-derived.cjs --folder "$PHASE" --apply
   671	  node .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/repair-derived.cjs --folder "$PKT" --apply
   672	  node .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/repair-derived.cjs --folder specs/system-speckit/041-write-recipe-fixes --apply
   673	  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PHASE" --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1
   674	  bash $S/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh "$PKT" --recursive --strict 2>&1 | grep 'RESULT:' | tail -1
   675	  bash $S/runtime/cli/rules/check-placeholders.sh "$PHASE"; echo "placeholder exit=$?"
   676	  node .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-goal/scripts/check-goal.cjs "$PKT" | tail -1
   677	  git status --porcelain > "$SC/baseline/status-end.txt"
   678	  diff "$SC/baseline/status-start.txt" "$SC/baseline/status-end.txt" | grep '^>' | sed 's/^> //' > "$SC/baseline/status-delta.txt"
   679	  cat "$SC/baseline/status-delta.txt"
   680	  awk '{print $2}' "$SC/baseline/status-delta.txt" | rg -v '^(\.skilled/(skills/system-spec-kit/(benchmark/reports/|SKILL\.md$|runtime/data/trigger-index\.json$|runtime/cli/retrieval/fixtures/(corpus-manifest|generation-diagnostics|phrase-variants)\.json$)|changelog/system-spec-kit/v2\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.md$)|specs/system-speckit/(graph-metadata\.json$|041-write-recipe-fixes/spec\.md$|054-weak-model-spec-authoring/(spec\.md$|goal\.md$|changelog/|009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/)))' | wc -l | tr -d ' '
   681	  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PKT/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[x\]'
   682	  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PKT/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[ \]' || true
   683	  sed -n '/ANCHOR:completion/,/\/ANCHOR:completion/p' "$PHASE/goal.md" | grep -c '^- \[x\]'
   684	  ```

    41	# Feature Specification: Make system-spec-kit templates and authoring docs followable by a DeepSeek V4.1 Flash agent swarm
    42	
    43	<!-- ANCHOR:metadata -->
    44	## 1. METADATA
    45	
    46	| Field | Value |
    47	|-------|-------|
    48	| **Level** | 2 |
    49	| **Priority** | P1 |
    50	| **Status** | Complete |
    51	| **Created** | 2026-10-23 |
    52	| **Branch** | `main` |
    53	| **Parent Spec** | `../spec.md` |
    54	| **Parent Packet** | system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring |
    55	| **Predecessor** | None |
    56	| **Successor** | None |
    57	| **Handoff Criteria** | All nine phases are Complete, `validate.sh --recursive --strict` on this packet prints `RESULT: PASSED`, and phase 009 reports a measured before and after for the five authoring metrics |
    58	<!-- /ANCHOR:metadata -->
    59	
    60	---
    61	
    62	<!-- ANCHOR:problem -->
    63	## 2. PROBLEM & PURPOSE
    64	
    65	### Problem Statement
    66	A weak-model worker (the benchmark subject is cli-devin `swe-2-max`, and single-change swarm lanes run on DeepSeek V4.1 Flash) that authors a spec packet from the system-spec-kit templates leaves scaffold hints in place and still passes `validate.sh --strict`. A fresh Level 2 scaffold holds 97 bracketed hint occurrences and validates clean. The hand-written examples the worker imitates carry leaked description text and stale lines. The authoring guidance is a 1,200-line template guide that a weak model cannot follow in one pass. Together these let a swarm produce packets that look finished and are not.
    67	
    68	### Purpose
    69	Make the templates, the examples, the validator and the authoring docs good enough that a weak-model swarm, measured on cli-devin `swe-2-max`, authors a valid, filled packet without a frontier model correcting it. Phase 001 measures the starting point and the last phase measures the result, so the claim rests on numbers.
    70	
    71	> **Phase-parent note:** This spec.md is the ONLY authored document at the parent level. All detailed planning, task breakdowns, checklists, and decisions live in the child phase folders listed in the Phase Documentation Map below. This keeps the parent from drifting stale as phases execute and pivot.
    72	<!-- /ANCHOR:problem -->
    73	
    74	---
    75	
    76	<!-- ANCHOR:scope -->
    77	## 3. SCOPE
    78	
    79	### In Scope
    80	- Repair the known template, example and reference defects, found by a census and fixed by exact text swap.
    81	- Gate unfilled placeholders on new docs only, through a template version marker.
    82	- Add a one-line fill instruction with a good and a weak example to each core template section.
    83	- Add a short authoring card and route the skill router to it.
    84	- Check and update the speckit and doctor commands and the doc-authoring agents such as markdown.
    85	- Align the playbook, catalog and READMEs of system-spec-kit, then run the playbooks in a disposable worktree.
    86	- Measure the authoring baseline before any change and again at the end.
    87	- One write-recipe fix, tracked as a child of the earlier write-recipe series.
    88	
    89	### Out of Scope
    90	- Any change to `sk-prompt`. All logic lives in system-spec-kit.
    91	- Any change that alters the verdict for an existing packet, because external-repo users would break on `/doctor:update`.
    92	- Measuring models other than cli-devin swe-2-max for the benchmark.
    93	- Pushing. Each phase ends in one local commit on the worktree branch and nothing is pushed.
    94	
    95	### Files to Change
    96	
    97	| File Path | Change Type | Phase | Description |
    98	|-----------|-------------|-------|-------------|
    99	| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/templates/examples/**` | Modify | 002, 004 | Example descriptions, stale lines, skeleton alignment |
   100	| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/references/**` | Modify | 002, 005, 006 | Style guide, checklist, template guide, new card |
   101	| `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/templates/core/*.tmpl` | Modify | 003, 004, 005 | Token form, v2.3 marker, fill guidance, task `Verify:` slot |

   109	
   110	### Launch preconditions
   111	
   112	The operator meets all four before the first phase runs. The conductor records each as met in `goal.md` section 4 and stops when one is not.
   113	
   114	1. **Workspace.** The operator chooses A) a git worktree or B) the current branch, following `sk-git`. The conductor never chooses. The chosen path becomes `WORKTREE` in `goal.md` section 4.
   115	2. **Provider login.** `devin auth status` reports a Devin login. A rate limit or refusal on cli-devin stops the run.
   116	3. **Lane authorization.** At most 3 parallel lanes and never two on one file. The operator has already given this.
   117	4. **Conductor placement.** The conductor is a Claude Code session. Execution and verification run on Sonnet 5.5 high subagents, plus cli-pi DeepSeek V4.1 Flash for single-change lanes, and every review runs on cli-devin `swe-2-max`, except the phase 009 closeout review, which the operator moved to cli-codex `gpt-6-luna` at max effort on the fast tier (approved 2026-10-25, recorded in the phase 009 `goal.md` section 4). The benchmark subject is cli-devin `swe-2-max`.

   123	## PHASE DOCUMENTATION MAP
   124	
   125	> This spec uses phased decomposition. Each phase is an independently executable child spec folder. All implementation details (plan, tasks, checklist, decisions, continuity) live inside the phase children.
   126	
   127	| Phase | Folder | Focus | Status |
   128	|-------|--------|-------|--------|
   129	| 1 | 001-template-drift-census-and-baseline/ | Census every template, example and reference defect into a verified ledger and measure the authoring baseline | Complete |
   130	| 2 | 002-template-and-example-defect-repair/ | Apply the ledger rows owned by this phase with a tested exact-match applier | Complete |
   131	| 3 | 003-versioned-placeholder-gate/ | Make unfilled `[YOUR_VALUE_HERE: ...]` tokens a Draft warning and a later error, for v2.3 docs only | Complete |
   132	| 4 | 004-template-section-fill-guidance/ | Add a fill instruction and a good and weak example to each core section, and guard example drift | Complete |
   133	| 5 | 005-weak-model-authoring-card/ | Write the authoring card, route the skill to it and guard the sync | Complete |
   134	| 6 | 006-speckit-command-and-agent-alignment/ | Check and update the speckit and doctor commands and the doc-authoring agents such as markdown for the new templates, gate and card | Complete |
   135	| 7 | 007-playbook-catalog-readme-alignment/ | Update the playbook, catalog and READMEs for the new behavior | Complete |
   136	| 8 | 008-playbook-run-in-worktree/ | Run every new or changed playbook scenario in a disposable worktree and report PASS or FAIL | Complete |
   137	| 9 | 009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout/ | Rerun the baseline, report the delta per metric and close the packet | Complete |
   138	
   139	### Phase Transition Rules
   140	
   141	- Each phase MUST pass `validate.sh` independently before the next phase begins
   142	- Parent spec tracks aggregate progress via this map
   143	- Use `/spec_kit:resume [parent-folder]/[NNN-phase]/` to resume a specific phase
   144	- Run `validate.sh --recursive` on parent to validate all phases as integrated unit
   145

   151	| 002-template-and-example-defect-repair | 003-versioned-placeholder-gate | Every owner-002 row no longer reproduces and the suite result matches the phase 001 start | Row evidence commands print nothing and the suite summary lines match `suite-start.txt` |
   152	| 003-versioned-placeholder-gate | 004-template-section-fill-guidance | Core hints are tokens, the marker is v2.3 and recursive strict counts are unchanged | The failing-first test passes and the before and after counts match |

   155	| 006-speckit-command-and-agent-alignment | 007-playbook-catalog-readme-alignment | Every speckit and doctor command and every doc-authoring agent names the card, the v2.3 token and the guard scripts where it describes authoring | The phase 006 audit table has no open row |
   156	| 007-playbook-catalog-readme-alignment | 008-playbook-run-in-worktree | Every new scenario file exists and the playbook index lists it | The playbook index lists each scenario id |
   157	| 008-playbook-run-in-worktree | 009-authoring-benchmark-and-closeout | Every scenario has a recorded PASS or an escalated FAIL | The report folder holds a row for each scenario |

    13	# v2.9.0.0, Templates a Weak Model Can Follow
    14	
    15	A weak model can now see what a template still needs, because every hint in the five core templates is a value token that fails validation once the packet leaves Draft. The four authoring intents also load a short card rather than the long guide, and the examples no longer teach the defects the old text carried. The before and after benchmark found fewer leftover hints, and its missing-anchor criterion failed three times before it passed, as described below.
    16	
    17	> Spec folder: `specs/system-speckit/054-weak-model-spec-authoring` (a phased packet of Level 3 phases)
    18	
    19	&nbsp;
    20	
    21	## What's New at a Glance
    22	
    23	- **An unfilled hint can no longer pass as finished.** A doc stamped v2.3 warns while the spec is Draft and fails strict validation at any other status.
    24	- **Every core section says how to fill it.** Each section carries a short instruction and a good and weak example.
    25	- **Authoring starts on one page.** The router loads a short card for the four authoring intents.
    26	- **The examples stop teaching the old defects.** Their descriptions, stale checklist lines and anchors now match the templates.
    27	- **Commands and agents name the new gate and the card.** A speckit or doctor run describes the same authoring path a worker follows.
    28	- **The playbook and catalog cover the new behavior.** A focused run of eight scenarios passed on a disposable worktree.
    29	- **The `L3+` label says what it means.** The fill note under each of the six `## L3+:` headings of the `tasks.md` template now opens with 'L3+ means Level 3 and above, so this section stays at Level 3.'
    30	- **The result is measured, and one criterion failed three times before it passed.** Three runs of one frozen Level 3 authoring task on each side gave a range for five metrics. On the first set (swe-2-max) leftover hints fell from a worst case of 1 to 0 and every run passed strict validation, but one final run deleted the six optional Level 3 verification sections of `tasks.md`, so the missing-anchor metric failed (6 against 0). After the label fix the final set was rerun on deepseek-v4-1-flash-1017-max against a new DeepSeek baseline. Leftover hints were 0 in all three runs and every run passed strict validation, but one run dropped three of the six sections (3 against 0), so the criterion failed again, and one run is contaminated because its session read the frozen vocabulary files. The operator then fixed an anchor rule (an optional section deleted as its fill note allows does not count) and three fresh runs in a disposable worktree with no benchmark files readable passed strict validation with no leftover hints, but run 1 deleted the required `fix-completeness` section, so the counted missing-anchor metric was 1 against 0 and the criterion failed a third time. The cause was fixed at the source: the fill note of the required `fix-completeness` section now says to keep the section and delete only rows. A second fresh set then passed the criterion, with counted missing anchors of 0 against 0, leftover hints of at most 1 against a baseline maximum of 1, and strict validation passing in every run.
    31	
    32	&nbsp;
    33	
    34	## Upgrade
    35	
    36	No migration required. Existing packets keep their verdict, because the token gate applies only to docs stamped v2.3. Scaffold a new packet to get the new templates and the authoring card.
